- **The "≈500-step Adam re-warming" distortion was bigger than estimated.** The warm-start docstring acknowledged ~500 steps of optimizer noise as the cost of fresh-Adam. In practice, the lost optimizer state appears to have triggered a much longer training-dynamics excursion that didn't resolve over 104k subsequent steps. Possible mechanisms (none verified; not separable without ablation):
  - **Catastrophic forgetting from gradient-noise dominance:** Fresh-Adam moments mean the first ~500 steps had unscaled gradient steps in directions that destabilized the carefully-balanced V_θ ordering at the deep tail. By the time Adam moments stabilized, the ordering was already eroded.
  - **target_net = copy of model collapsed bootstrap signal:** Mid-training, target_net lags by ~500 steps. At warm-start it was identical to value_net, so the Bellman bootstrap target became "predict your own current prediction" for the first sync interval. The network may have over-fit to its current predictions, locking in a degraded state.
  - **K_max=20 distribution shift on a near-converged net:** The pretrained net at step 16k was already close to the K=20 stationary distribution. Continued training on the same K_max with fresh optimizer state may have drifted the network toward a different (worse) stationary point — the loss is stable, but it's the loss of the WRONG attractor.
  - All three are speculative. The data point is the regression. The mechanism is the open question.
- **macro_v_star_mae is now triple-confirmed as the wrong production metric for cycle-to-cycle decisions.** Three runs, three different relationships to capability:
  - smoke: macro flat through training tail; capability quietly improved
  - full_train: macro U-shaped; capability climbed monotonically through the U
  - warm_continue: macro flat in 0.64-0.78 band; capability collapsed by 50+pp at d=14
  - **Decision recorded:** beam_eval at d=10..14 (especially d=13, d=14) is the production verdict. macro_v_star_mae is one calibration metric — useful for "is the network learning at all," useless for "is this cycle a net improvement." Always cross-check on capability before accepting a cycle.
- **Decision recorded: full resumability infra (preserve Adam moments + target_net state across save/load) is now blocking, not optional.** Tomorrow's planned "resumability block" was sized at half a day; this run's collapse is the reason that work matters. The minimal warm-start can't be considered "training continuation" — it's a destabilizing reinitialization with smarter starting weights, and for value-iteration on a near-converged network, that destabilization is destructive.
- **Decision recorded: don't run additional warm-start experiments until full resumability lands.** The minimal warm-start path stays in `run.py` for emergency use (e.g. resume after a kernel crash where some loss is acceptable), but no more "compare warm-continue to fresh" experiments — they'd be measuring the warm-start design distortion, not anything scientific about training trajectories.
- **Decision recorded: the instrumentation cleanup commits (A/B/C/D) all landed and are independently valuable** even though the warm-continue experiment they enabled regressed. wandb namespacing, banded aggregates, zero-pad d-keys, step-axis as default x-axis — all good independently. The `--checkpoint-steps` flag for post_run_beam_eval is a generally-useful addition for any future run with many checkpoints. None of these need to be reverted.
- **Open question for next block: when EXACTLY did capability begin eroding?** We have checkpoints every 2k steps (52 of them) but only beam-eval'd 7. Step 20k (4k post-warm-start) already shows d=14 down 14pp from source — meaning the regression started immediately. Beam-eval'ing net_step_18000.pt and net_step_22000.pt (or even denser, every 2k from step 18k → 30k) would tell us whether the collapse began in the first 2k steps (Adam moments thrashing) or built gradually.
- **Open question: would full resumability (preserved Adam + preserved target_net lag) actually preserve the training trajectory?** Answer requires the resumability infra + a re-run; that's the next experiment.
- **No NaN, no MPS errors, training surface ran 7.3h cleanly.** Engineering surface is solid; it's the warm-start *design* that misbehaves, not the training loop.
- **Followups (notes for next block / backlog):**
  - **Resumability infra (now PRIORITY).** Preserve `optimizer.state_dict()`, `target_net.state_dict()`, RNG state, step counter, and curriculum state in a single `train_state_*.pt` checkpoint format. Add `--resume <run_dir>` to append-mode metrics + same wandb run via `wandb.init(resume="must", id=...)`. Add `--branch-from <checkpoint>` for the cross-run-branching case. Half-day work. Verify with a re-run from full_train net_final.pt + full state — does d=14 capability hold/climb? Falsifies or confirms the "Adam state loss caused collapse" hypothesis.
  - **Dense beam-eval over the warm_continue collapse window.** Run `--checkpoint-steps 18000,20000,22000,24000,26000,28000` to nail down whether the regression started in the first 2k post-warm steps (Adam thrashing) or built gradually across the run.
  - **Bounded V* oracle to K=8** (existing backlog item, priority confirmed by V*=1..6 being fully saturated at this scale).
  - `smaller_net.yaml` comparison run (still on the bench for a future evening).
  - Once resumability infra lands and the warm-continue re-run confirms or falsifies the mechanism, decide whether the original full_train trajectory (d=14 climbing 0.48 → 0.78 over 11k steps) was approaching a local maximum or had real headroom further out.
- **Drive-by:** `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` gained a `--checkpoint-steps` flag (commit `00acc3f`). Generally useful — any run with many intermediate checkpoints can subsample.
**Commits:** `1bd2324` (block open), `c616440` (A — wandb cleanup), `57efb5f` (B — early-stop removal + step-axis), `d1b109e` (C — smaller_net.yaml), `53a5236` (D1 — warm-start CLI), `f154d7a` (D2 — warm_continue.yaml), `207ba8a` (LOG amend with launch info), `00acc3f` (post_run_beam_eval --checkpoint-steps flag), `93fb2a9` (analysis pipeline extended + HTML regenerated), `6fbd25e` (run artifacts), `<this commit>` (Outcome).

## 2026-05-06 — M8 full 3x3 training run: K_max=20, 100k steps ✅ done
**Goal:** First full-budget 3x3 DAVI training run. K_max=20 flat from random init (NOT warm-start), 100,000 steps, same arch as smoke (`[5120, 1024] × 4 BN`). Goal is to see how far a single long run pushes 3x3 capability — does macro_v_star_mae descend further past smoke's plateau at 0.73, and does deep-d beam capability climb past smoke's d=10..14 numbers? Early-stop on (patience=12 evals, warmup=4, min_delta=0.001) — fires if signal plateaus.
**Milestone:** M8 phase 3 ([plan](plans/m8-3x3-davi.md))
**Approach:** Branch `m8-3x3-full-train` from main HEAD `aecb416`. Two atomic sub-blocks:
- **H4 eval re-seed fix**: `value_eval` currently advances a single `eval_generator` across calls; per-V* MAE bounces eval-to-eval as a result. Fix: re-seed per call from `seed + step` (or equivalent deterministic-from-seed scheme). Same eval set every step → smooth trajectory readable across 200 evals.
- **Full training run**: author `experiments/davi-3x3/configs/full_train.yaml` (K_max=20, n_steps=100000, target_sync_interval=500, eval_every=500, checkpoint_every=5000, batch_size=4096, lr=0.001, arch [5120, 1024] × 4 BN, early-stop patience=12 / warmup=4 / min_delta=0.001). Launch via `experiments/davi-3x3/run.py --config configs/full_train.yaml`. Estimated wall ~6-7h at smoke pace (235 ms/step).
**Next:** Block closed. Branch merges to main; next block (`m8-instrumentation-cleanup`) addresses the four instrumentation followups + smaller-net comparison rerun.
**In progress:**
- Block opened. Branch `m8-3x3-full-train` from main HEAD `aecb416`.
- **H4 eval re-seed fix done (`7d197b5`):** `value_eval` constructs a fresh `torch.Generator` per call seeded from `config.seed + 17` instead of advancing a shared generator across calls. Eval set now identical across training steps (only the network weights vary call-to-call). Tested.
- **full_train.yaml authored (`9e4a34e`):** K_max=20 flat, n_steps=100000, target_sync_interval=500, eval_every=500, checkpoint_every=5000, batch_size=4096, lr=0.001, arch `[5120, 1024] × 4 BN`, early-stop patience=12 / warmup=4 / min_delta=0.001, device=mps, seed=0.
- **Run launched + completed (`b357mdf64`):** wall **67 min** on M4 Max. **Early-stop fired at step 16,000** (eval-32 of 200 planned), well short of the 100k cap. Run dir `experiments/davi-3x3/runs/20260507T043533Z_full_train/`. wandb: `griffin-enterprises/rubik-3x3/runs/b357mdf64`. macro_v_star_mae trajectory: descended steeply 5.39 → 0.6393 over steps 500–10,000 (best at step 10k), then a **U-shape**: rose to ~0.92 at step 12,000, recovered to 0.65 at step 15,000, sat at 0.76 at step 16,000 when patience window of 12 evals from the step-10k best closed. Final checkpoints saved at steps 5k / 10k / 15k / final (= 16k).
- **Beam-eval analysis on all 4 checkpoints (subagent landed `bc620b1` + `1bfd9e7` + `7fd0f16`):** ran `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` on each checkpoint, then extended `experiments/davi-3x3/analysis/` for the full_train schema and regenerated `experiments/davi-3x3/results/error_trajectories.html`. **Key finding — the U-shape in macro is calibration drift, NOT capability regression.** Beam (width=256) at deep walk-depths CLIMBED MONOTONICALLY through the U-shape:

  | step | d=10 | d=12 | d=14 |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 5k | 0.99 | 0.89 | 0.48 |
  | 10k (macro best) | 1.00 | 0.98 | 0.59 |
  | 15k | 1.00 | 0.98 | 0.69 |
  | 16k (final) | 1.00 | 0.99 | **0.78** |

  d=14 climbed **+19pp** over the steps macro called "stop." Patience killed a training run that was breaking out at the deep tail.
- **V*=1..6 panel saturated at 100% on every checkpoint** (bounded oracle K=6 is now uninformative for 3x3 capability discrimination at this training scale). Backlog item: extend bounded oracle to K=8 or K=10 — current backlog already has "K=8 V* expansion" item from M8 P1; promote priority.
- **Bug fix in `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` (`bc620b1`):** the script previously labeled `net_final.pt` at `config.n_steps` (= 100,000), but under early-stop the file actually corresponds to step 16,000. New `_final_step_from_metrics` reads the highest step from `metrics.jsonl` and uses that as the final's label, falling back to `n_steps` if metrics absent. Trajectories now place the final checkpoint at its true step on the x-axis.
- **3 commits from beam-eval subagent:** `bc620b1` (analyze + capture extended for full_train + final-step bug fix), `1bfd9e7` (regenerate results.md + error_trajectories.html), `7fd0f16` (artifacts: config + metrics + post-hoc beam eval).

**Outcome:**
- **Headline: macro_v_star_mae is the wrong early-stop signal at K_max >> K_oracle.** The bounded oracle K=6 only measures shallow calibration (V*=1..6). At K_max=20 the network's natural prediction range expands far beyond d=6 — pred_mean rises into the 5+ region by mid-training as the network learns to score deep states with appropriately deep predictions. **macro rising at deep training depths is by design, not regression.** The U-shape recovery (step 12k peak → step 15k 0.65) is consistent with the network re-stabilizing its shallow calibration after a deep-end push, and beam capability never wavered through the supposed "regression." **Decision: remove early-stop wiring in next block** (keep DAVIConfig fields for backwards compat).
- **Cycle-4 inversion redux: macro alone is never the verdict.** This run reproduces the project's core methodology lesson (CLAUDE.md "Evaluating training cycles correctly") in the opposite direction from the smoke's surprise: smoke had macro flat while capability climbed; this run has macro plateau-then-rise while capability climbs +19pp at d=14. **Decision recorded:** beam-eval at d=10..14 is the production search-quality verdict; macro_v_star_mae is one calibration metric among many.
- **V*=1..6 panel is dead weight at this training scale.** All four checkpoints solve V*=1..6 at 100%. Useful in M8 P1/P2 (random-init networks scored ~0%); now uninformative for cycle-to-cycle discrimination. **Decision recorded:** the K=6 bounded oracle still has value as a sanity ground truth (V*[solved]=0 invariant; per-layer state count) but its capability-eval value is exhausted. Need a deeper-V* ground-truth strategy — bumping to K=8 (which P1b's 4.8s build estimate suggests is cheap) or alternative deep-state ground truth (e.g. a known-optimal solver invocation on a small set). Backlog already carries "K=8 V* expansion warm-started from K=6" — promoted.
- **Patience=12 fires on a wrong metric in this regime.** Removing rather than tweaking. The plateau detector itself is fine (8 unit tests in `test_early_stop.py` still pass); it's the choice of `macro_v_star_mae` as the signal that's broken at K_max=20.
- **No NaN / no MPS errors on the 67-min run.** Training surface scales from 10k (smoke) → 16k (early-stopped full_train) without instability. Loss decreased monotonically.
- **Decisions left for the next block (NOT acted on this block):**
  - **Resumability infra**: full training-state checkpoints (`net_state` + `target_net_state` + `optimizer_state` + `step` — already in place per P2a's checkpoint format; just unused on launch), `--resume` flag (already wired in `run.py`), `--branch` flag for resuming with parameter changes, wandb run continuity. Half-day work, deferred to its own block tomorrow rather than rolled into the instrumentation cleanup.
  - **K=8 oracle extension**: existing backlog item, priority promoted by this run's V*=1..6 saturation. Warm-start from K=6 cache.
  - **Comparison data point at smaller arch**: smaller_net config (`[2048, 1024]` × 4 BN) for an overnight comparison run. NOT a tier-N experiment — just a second data point to break the n=1 anchor on `[5120, 1024]`.
- **Drive-by: `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` final-step labeling bug fixed (`bc620b1`).** Was labeling `net_final.pt` at `config.n_steps` regardless of early-stop. Now reads the highest step from metrics.jsonl. Affected anyone running post-hoc beam eval on an early-stopped run.
**Commits:** `a531ffe` (block open), `7d197b5` (H4 eval re-seed fix), `9e4a34e` (full_train.yaml config), `bc620b1` (analyze pipeline extended + final-step bug fix), `1bfd9e7` (regenerate results.md + error_trajectories.html), `7fd0f16` (artifacts: config + metrics + post-hoc beam eval), `d4d928a` (Outcome).

## 2026-05-06 — M8 P2: smoke training with early-stop infra ✅ done
**Goal:** Train one 3x3 DAVI run end-to-end with proper instrumentation. Verify (a) the wired-up training loop works on 3x3 — loss decreases, no NaN, no MPS errors; (b) `macro_v_star_mae` evolves downward over training (network is learning the value function); (c) sync-period-keyed early-stop behaves sensibly on a real trajectory; (d) post-training beam evals produce our first 3x3 capability data points. **Goal is NOT acceptance-grade champion training** — this is smoke. Phase 3+ replans against Phase 2 outcomes.
**Milestone:** M8 phase 2 ([plan](plans/m8-3x3-davi.md))
**Approach:** Branch `m8-3x3-smoke-train` from main HEAD `fa0d648`. Two atomic sub-blocks on the branch.

**P2a** — Early-stop infra + value_eval wiring + W&B project switch:
- Add 5 required fields to `DAVIConfig` in `src/rubik/training/config.py`: `early_stop_enabled: bool`, `early_stop_metric: str`, `early_stop_patience_evals: int`, `early_stop_min_evals: int`, `early_stop_min_delta: float`. No defaults (project rule). YAML round-trip + tests. **Constraint at config-load:** if `early_stop_enabled=true`, require `eval_every == target_sync_interval` (or clean multiple) — enforces patience-in-evals = patience-in-syncs. Errors on misalignment.
- Backfill the 5 new fields into all historical `experiments/davi-2x2/runs/*/config.yaml` snapshots (schema-additive; flat behavior = `early_stop_enabled: false`).
- Wire `value_eval` from `experiments/davi-3x3/eval.py` into `experiments/davi-3x3/run.py` at every `eval_every`. Reverse the P1c `# P2a:` markers. Track `macro_v_star_mae` history; fire early-stop when criteria meet (write `event=early_stop`, save final checkpoint, exit cleanly).
- Switch `experiments/davi-3x3/run.py` wandb project default from `rubik` → `rubik-3x3`. Auto-tag wandb runs with `cube=3x3`, `phase=smoke`.
- Tests for early-stop logic on synthetic metric histories: improving / flat / decreasing-then-flat / never-improves.

**P2b** — Smoke run + post-training beam eval + writeup:
- Author `experiments/davi-3x3/configs/smoke.yaml` per plan: `body_widths: [5120, 1024]` × 4 BN residual blocks (hand-picked guess, NOT borrowed), `max_scramble_depth: 8`, `batch_size: 4096`, `n_steps: 10000` hardcap, `learning_rate: 0.001`, `target_sync_interval: 500`, `eval_every: 500` (aligned to sync), `checkpoint_every: 2500`, early-stop `patience=12 / warmup=4 / min_delta=0.001`, `device: mps`.
- Run training: `uv run python experiments/davi-3x3/run.py --config configs/smoke.yaml`. Expect either n_steps cap or early-stop firing on plateau. Watch wandb live.
- Post-training: invoke `beam_eval_walk` + `beam_eval_v_star` on `net_final.pt`; write `runs/<run>/results/beam_eval.json` + summary metrics to wandb under `eval/beam_walk/*` + `eval/beam_v_star/*`.
- Optional follow-up: run `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` on intermediate checkpoints to produce the beam-trajectory chart.
- Writeup `experiments/davi-3x3/results/results.md` with the project's intuition convention (Observations → Hypotheses with verification plans → Open questions). Hand-written `intuition.md` companion that `analyze.py` appends.

**Phase 2 acceptance gate (from plan §Phase 2 acceptance gate):** (1) DAVIConfig extended + tests + load-time validation; (2) smoke run completes (cap or early-stop) with no NaN/MPS errors; (3) `macro_v_star_mae` trajectory visibly downward, not flat from step 0, not exploding; (4) post-training beam evals yield per-V* + per-walk-depth solve rates; (5) results writeup includes intuition section; (6) wandb run shows up under `rubik-3x3` (NOT `rubik`) with three panel groups populated.

**Next:** Phase 2 acceptance gate met end-to-end. Both sub-blocks done. Ready to close this 🟡 block — but holding for user signal per the new "use descriptive names + don't drive process autonomously" feedback. When closed, branch merges to main and a new block opens to address H1/H2/H4 follow-ups (longer training run; eval-set fixed-from-seed; possibly tighter early-stop patience).
**In progress:**
- Block opened. Branch `m8-3x3-smoke-train` from main HEAD `fa0d648` (the merge commit closing P1). cc-process commit-gate honored: P1 fully merged before opening P2. Plan stays at `plans/m8-3x3-davi.md` — no plan revisions needed for P2a/P2b kickoff.
- **Early-stop infra done (`9ac07c9`):** five new required `DAVIConfig` fields (`early_stop_enabled` / `early_stop_metric` / `early_stop_patience_evals` / `early_stop_min_evals` / `early_stop_min_delta`); load-time validation enforces `eval_every` is a clean multiple of `target_sync_interval` when early-stop is on. `EarlyStopMonitor` lives in `src/rubik/training/early_stop.py` (spec-agnostic per CLAUDE.md naming rule); 8 synthetic-history tests cover monotonic-decreasing / flat-from-zero / decreasing-then-flat / sub-`min_delta` / warmup-blocks / state-snapshot / `min_delta=0` strict-less-than / invalid-args. `experiments/davi-3x3/run.py` reverses every `# P2a:` marker — loads bounded oracle dict, deterministic eval generator (`seed+17`), calls `value_eval` at every `eval_every` and logs the flat dict as one `event="eval"` record, runs `EarlyStopMonitor.update(macro_v_star_mae)` (NaN-guarded), fires `event="early_stop"` cleanly. wandb default project flipped `rubik` → `rubik-3x3` with auto-tags `cube=3x3` `phase=smoke`. Backfilled the 5 fields into 9 historical `experiments/davi-2x2/runs/*/config.yaml` snapshots (schema-additive; flat behavior). Test count 520 → 538 (+18). 15 files changed.
- **Smoke training run done (`2b6814d`):** ran `experiments/davi-3x3/configs/smoke.yaml` (hand-picked arch `[5120, 1024] × 4 BN` per the plan, K_max=8, 10k steps cap). 39-min wall on M4 Max @ 235 ms/step. Run dir `experiments/davi-3x3/runs/20260506T203408Z_smoke/`. wandb: https://wandb.ai/griffin-enterprises/rubik-3x3/runs/4m9el9ws. macro_v_star_mae **descended 3.48 → 0.73 over steps 500–7,500** (5x improvement), then **plateaued in the 0.73–0.79 band** for the remaining 5,000 steps. Best at step 7,500 (0.7340 corrected). Early-stop did not fire — 12-eval patience window extends past the 10k cap from any best.
- **Bug found + fixed in same commit:** `value_eval`'s `macro_v_star_mae` averaged across V\*=0..K when V\*=0 happened to populate (walks of length ≥ ~6 occasionally return to solved by random-walk redundancy). V\*=0 is terminal-value-calibration, not "V\* prediction error on non-trivial states" — including it inflated the macro by ~25% on 3 of 20 evals (steps 2,000 / 5,500 / 10,000) and made the headline scalar inconsistent eval-to-eval. The "regression at step 10,000" in the recorded values was almost entirely this artifact (recorded 0.88 vs corrected 0.75). Fix: exclude V\*=0 from the macro. Test added. Test count 538 → 539.
- **Beam capability surprise (cycle-4 inversion):** focused beam eval on step 7,500 vs step 10,000 (n_per_depth=50 / n_per_layer=100 / beam_width=128) — both checkpoints solve **100% with optimal length** on every bounded-oracle V\* layer (d=1..6), and step 10,000 is **better than step 7,500 at the deep walk tail**: +16pp at d=12, +10pp at d=14, +4pp at d=11. The training continued to push capability forward past step 7,500 even as macro stayed flat in the bulk. **Falsifies the cycle-4-style hypothesis** (where macro improved while capability regressed); here macro stays flat (corrected) while capability improves. Documented in `experiments/davi-3x3/intuition.md` §H1.
- **Phase 2 acceptance gate (per plan §Phase 2):** ✅ all six criteria met. (1) DAVIConfig 5 fields + tests + alignment validation; (2) smoke run completes without NaN/MPS errors via n_steps cap; (3) `macro_v_star_mae` trajectory visibly downward (5x); (4) post-training beam evals produce per-V\* + per-walk-depth solve rates; (5) results writeup with intuition section per project convention (`results.md` + `intuition.md`); (6) wandb run under `rubik-3x3` with three panel groups populated.
- **Deviation noted (initially):** `experiments/davi-3x3/analysis/analyze.py` and `render_error_trajectories.py` were scaffolded for the 2x2 metric schema; the new 3x3 `value_eval` emits a different schema. Initially deferred — but the user flagged the missing HTML, so the renderer + analyzer were ported in `47d9c06`.
- **Renderer + analyzer ported (`47d9c06`):** `render_error_trajectories.py` adapted for the 3x3 schema — corrected_macro line (recomputed per-record from `v_star_mae/d{1..6}`, V*=0 contamination bypassed), per-V*-layer MAE small multiples (d=1..6, bounded oracle range), per walk-depth `pred_mean` + `pred_std` bands (Shallow/Mid/Deep per project convention), beam capability dodged-bar panels from `beam_eval_focused.json`. `analyze.py` schema-adapted to register the smoke run and emit corrected_macro + per-V*-layer summary. Heatmap + solve-length histograms kept as graceful placeholders (heatmap needs ≥2 runs; histograms need extending `capture_solve_histograms.py` for the 3x3 schema). `experiments/davi-3x3/results/error_trajectories.html` is now a 72 KB chart-rich page openable with `open <path>`. Test count unchanged at 539 (renderer/analyzer aren't unit-tested).
- **Note: analyzer doesn't yet append intuition.md to results.md.** The 2x2 convention is `analyze.py` reads `intuition.md` and appends it; the 3x3 analyzer port didn't wire that. Currently `intuition.md` lives standalone. **Followup (small):** wire intuition-append in the next 3x3 cycle.

**Outcome:**
- **Phase 2 acceptance gate met end-to-end.** All six criteria (DAVIConfig + tests, run completes without errors, macro_v_star_mae trajectory visibly downward, post-training beam evals produce per-V* + per-walk-depth solve rates, results writeup with intuition section, wandb run under `rubik-3x3` with three panel groups). Five sub-blocks committed cleanly: P2a infra, smoke training + bug fix + writeup, LOG record, renderer port.
- **Smoke training works on 3x3 end-to-end.** 39 min wall, 235 ms/step, no NaN/MPS errors. Network learns: corrected `macro_v_star_mae` 3.48 → 0.73 over steps 500–7,500 (5x improvement), then plateaus 0.73–0.79 through step 10k. Early-stop did not fire (12-eval patience exceeds 10k cap from any best). 15.3M-param net at `[5120, 1024] × 4 BN`. **Decision: this is smoke quality, NOT a champion baseline** — the training surface is validated, but neither the architecture nor the K_max=8 distribution has been earned through the full T0 sweep.
- **Bug found + fixed in `value_eval` (commit `2b6814d`).** `macro_v_star_mae` averaged across V*=0..K when V*=0 happened to populate (rare; walks of length ≥ ~6 occasionally return to solved by random-walk redundancy). V*=0 captures terminal-value calibration — a different quality from "V* prediction error on non-trivial states" — and including it inflated the macro by ~25% on 3 of 20 evals (steps 2,000 / 5,500 / 10,000). The "regression at step 10,000" in the recorded values (0.88 vs 0.73 best) was almost entirely this artifact (corrected = 0.75, only +0.015 vs best). **Fix:** exclude V*=0 from the macro mean. Test added. The historical metrics.jsonl is unchanged (record); future runs record the corrected scalar directly.
- **Cycle-4 inversion finding (recorded as a future-relevant lesson).** Focused beam eval (n_per_depth=50, n_per_layer=100, beam_width=128) on net_step_7500 vs net_final compared the BEST-MAE checkpoint against the FINAL: per-V* layer (d=1..6) both solve **100% at optimal length**; per walk-depth (d=10..14) step 10,000 is **better** than step 7,500 — +16pp at d=12, +10pp at d=14, +4pp at d=11. **The training continued to push capability forward at the deep walk tail past step 7,500 even as macro stayed flat in the bulk.** This INVERTS the M5-followup cycle-4 pattern (where macro improved while capability regressed; CLAUDE.md "Evaluating training cycles correctly"). Both directions exist; macro alone is never the verdict — beam capability must cross-check.
- **Decision (recorded): the analysis pipeline extends per project convention, never parallel-builds.** When the user asked "where's the HTML?", the right answer was extending `render_error_trajectories.py` for the 3x3 schema, NOT writing a one-off chart file. The same convention from CLAUDE.md "Cycle reporting pipeline" that was lesson-of-cycle-4 (M5-followup) also applied here.
- **Decision (recorded): early-stop patience of 12 evals is too generous for a 10k-step cap.** The corrected trajectory plateaued at step 7,500; with patience=12, stop wouldn't fire until step 13,500. For Phase 3+ smokes at this n_steps, patience should be 4–6. For longer runs (20k+), 12 is fine.
- **Decisions left for next cycle (NOT acted on this block):**
  - **H2 (medium-high):** training hasn't converged at 10k — beam capability still climbing at d=12, d=14; pred_std still rising. Verification: 30k-step run at the same config.
  - **H4 (medium):** `value_eval` advances `eval_generator` each call instead of re-seeding; per-V* MAE bouncing eval-to-eval is consistent with eval-set noise. Verification: re-seed each eval call, compare trajectory smoothness.
  - **K_max ceiling test:** does K_max=8 cap learning at the deep walk tail? Verification: K_max sweep at fixed step budget.
  - **T0 capacity sweep:** the `[5120, 1024] × 4 BN` arch was hand-picked (not earned). When the next 3x3 cycle isn't a smoke, T0 sweep earns the real config.
- **Followups noted as backlog or next-block scope (not opened as their own blocks):**
  - Wire intuition-append into 3x3 `analyze.py` (preserves intuition section across regenerations).
  - Extend `capture_solve_histograms.py` for the 3x3 schema so the histogram + heatmap chart sections turn on.
  - K=8 V\* expansion warm-started from K=6 (cheap given the 4.8s build).
  - 2x2 W&B project rename to `rubik-2x2`.
- **Drive-by (small):** backfilled 5 new early-stop fields into 9 historical `experiments/davi-2x2/runs/*/config.yaml` snapshots (schema-additive; flat behavior). One snapshot also got the older missing curriculum fields. All 9 verified to load.
- **Test count over the block: 520 → 539 (+19).** New tests: 8 for `EarlyStopMonitor` synthetic histories, 5 for `DAVIConfig` round-trip + missing-field + alignment-rule, 5 for new value_eval shape (existing P1c tests were 5 → 6 when we added the V*=0 exclusion test in P2b).
**Commits:** `fa4bf97` (block open), `9ac07c9` (early-stop infra + run.py wiring), `2b6814d` (smoke training run + bug fix + writeup), `5cbc87d` (LOG record), `47d9c06` (renderer + analyzer port), `<this commit>` (Outcome).

## 2026-05-06 — M8 P1: 3x3 eval scaffold + bounded V* sanity oracle ✅ done
**Goal:** Stand up `experiments/davi-3x3/` mirroring the 2x2 layer, build a bounded BFS V* oracle at K=6 (~1M states, ~60–80MB cache, ~30–120s build) as a sanity-check ground truth, and freeze a 1400-state eval set (100 × 14 walk-depths). Three sub-blocks (P1a/b/c) stack on this branch. Goal isn't deep-eval coverage — that's accepted as a permanent gap on 3x3 (state space is 4.3×10¹⁹). Goal IS confirming the eval pipeline runs end-to-end on 3x3 before any training cycle.
**Milestone:** M8 phase 1 ([plan](plans/m8-3x3-davi.md))
**Approach:** Branch `m8-3x3-eval-scaffold` from main HEAD `d842f6a`. Three atomic sub-blocks: **P1a** scaffold `experiments/davi-3x3/` mirroring `davi-2x2/` (run.py, configs/, runs/, analysis/, results/, README.md, eval.py, build_eval_set_3x3.py, calibrate_step_time_3x3.py); first verify `davi-2x2/analysis/*.py` is spec-agnostic — fix at source if any `CUBE_2X2` literals exist, do NOT copy-modify into davi-3x3. **P1b** new module `src/rubik/oracle/v_star_bounded_3x3.py` (size-suffixed) doing reverse-BFS from `CUBE_3X3.solved_state` layer-by-layer up to K=6; cache to `data/v_star_bounded_3x3_k6.npz`; tests in `tests/oracle/test_v_star_bounded_3x3.py` cover total state count = 983,926, V*[solved]=0, random-walk-d∈{1..6} round-trip lands on V*≤d state, identity sanity (move shifts V* by ±1 or 0). **P1c** `experiments/davi-3x3/build_eval_set_3x3.py` generates a frozen eval set (1400 states, fields: states uint8[14,100,54] / walk_depth int8[14,100] / v_star int8[14,100] with -1 sentinel for d>6); `eval.py` loads it and reports per-depth solve rate against a random-init `ValueNet` (sanity: ~0% everywhere). Each sub-block atomic commit, passing tests, ruff clean.
**Next:** P1a + P1b + P1c all done. Phase 1 acceptance gate met: three-function eval API live, scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py exists, tests green (515 → 520). Ready to close this 🟡 block and open new block `m8-3x3-smoke-train` for Phase 2 (early-stop infra + smoke training). Pending user signal via `/cc-handoff`.
**In progress:**
- Block opened. Branch `m8-3x3-eval-scaffold` from main HEAD `d842f6a`. Plan document at `plans/m8-3x3-davi.md` (committed on main as `d842f6a`).
- **2026-05-06 design pivots (per conversation; plan doc updated):** P1c reshaped from "1400-state frozen npz" to a lean three-function eval API. `value_eval` (live during training, drives early-stop via `macro_v_star_mae` against the d≤6 oracle subset) + `beam_eval_walk` (post-training, forever capability eval) + `beam_eval_v_star` (post-training, ground truth while oracle exists). Beam runs **post-training only** — beam cost would slow training. Greedy = `beam_width=1` special case, no separate impl. Phase 2 reshaped: T0 capacity calibration **deferred to backlog**; arch hand-picked at `[5120, 1024] × 4 bn` (acknowledged guess). Early-stop infra: 5 DAVIConfig fields keyed on **target-sync periods** (12 patience, 4 warmup, 0.001 min_delta on `macro_v_star_mae`). W&B project = **`rubik-3x3`** (separate from 2x2 `rubik`). `checkpoint_every` saves intermediates for post-hoc trajectory via `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py`. Backlog additions in ROADMAP: K=8 V\* expansion (warm-start from K=6), T0 calibration sweep, 2x2 wandb project rename.
- **P1b done (`11cac57`):** `src/rubik/oracle/v_star_bounded_3x3.py` — bounded BFS V* at K=6, mirrors `v_star_2x2`'s API shape (compute / load / save / lookup, with `_arrays` and `_batch` variants). **Deviation from 2x2:** dropped 24-rotation canonicalization — 3x3 centers are positionally fixed under QTM, so raw `state.tobytes()` is canonical for this encoding (documented in module docstring). Build wall time **4.8s on M4 Max** (well under plan's 30-120s estimate — batched fancy-index expansion via `apply_all_moves` is fast; means K=8 would also be cheap if we need it later). Per-layer counts match the well-known QTM table exactly: 1 / 12 / 114 / 1068 / 10011 / 93840 / 878880, total **983,926**. Cache **14.9 MB** at `data/v_star_bounded_3x3_k6.npz` (npz compresses int8/uint8 well — was estimated at 60-80MB). Cache **gitignored** per v_star_2x2 pattern; rebuilt by `scripts/build_v_star_bounded_3x3.py`. Tests in `tests/oracle/test_v_star_bounded_3x3.py` (15 new tests, suite 500 → 515): layer count sentinel (983,926); V*[solved]=0; random-walk d∈{1..6} round-trip → V*≤d invariant; move-shift sanity (V* shifts by ±1 or 0 per QTM move on V*∈{1..5} samples; k=6 children may exit the bounded oracle, expected). Module-scoped pytest fixture loads oracle once.
- **P1c done (`619e90c`):** Three-function eval API live in `experiments/davi-3x3/eval.py`. `value_eval` (live every sync — per-walk-depth pred stats + per-V*-layer MAE against bounded oracle for d≤K, dynamically discovered so K=8 later just works; reports `macro_v_star_mae` driving early-stop). `beam_eval_walk` (post-training random-walk × beam, forever capability eval). `beam_eval_v_star` (post-training V*-stratified × beam, ground truth while oracle exists; skips V*=0). New helper `sample_states_at_v_star_3x3` in `v_star_bounded_3x3.py` (no rotate option — 3x3 isn't canonicalized). New `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` iterates `net_step_*.pt` + `net_final.pt` in a run dir, writes trajectory JSON; empty-checkpoints contract = writes `{}` exit 0. New tests at `tests/experiments/test_eval_3x3.py` (5 tests; suite **515 → 520**, +5). Surgical run.py patch: old `eval_against_v_star` / `greedy_solve` call sites + `eval_states_dev` / `eval_solve_generator` commented out with `# P2a: replaced by value_eval — see plans/m8-3x3-davi.md` markers (full wiring is P2a's job; `--help` parses cleanly, imports work). Decisions made on the fly: (a) `value_eval` accepts `oracle_dict` not `oracle_arrays` for batch lookup ergonomics; (b) `beam_eval_v_star` uses a separate forward pass for per-V* MAE rather than fishing predictions out of beam internals; (c) post_run_beam_eval emits `{}` for empty dirs so downstream readers don't special-case missing files. 6 files changed (+811 / -140). `uv run pytest -x -q` 5.58s clean; ruff clean.
- **P1a done (`9899a8f`):** `experiments/davi-3x3/` scaffolded mirroring davi-2x2. Audit confirmed every 2x2-specific hardcode is legitimately category-(a) (CUBE_2X2, v_star_2x2 paths, [4096,1024] arch, depth-14 QTM diameter, RUNS lists) — no source parameterization needed; davi-3x3 gets parallel CUBE_3X3 / bounded V*  / empty-RUNS / TBD-arch values. 14 new files, 1939 insertions. `run.py` / `eval.py` import `CUBE_3X3` and point at davi-3x3 paths; eval.py docstring notes 3x3 eval-set depth-bin = `walk_depth` (not V*). `calibrate_step_time_3x3.py` is a stub raising `NotImplementedError` until P2a fills the grid. Analysis layer (analyze / capture_solve_histograms / render_error_trajectories / audit_sampler) ported with empty `RUNS` lists; arch constants in capture_solve_histograms (`BODY_WIDTHS`, `N_RESIDUAL_BLOCKS`, `NORMALIZATION`) raise `NotImplementedError` if loaded — to be earned at P2a/P2b. `audit_sampler.py` adds an "overflow bucket" for V*>K=6 (2x2 enumerates the whole space; 3x3 can't). `build_eval_set_3x3.py` deliberately NOT scaffolded — that's P1c. Smoke tests all green: `import run/eval` clean, analysis scripts run on empty `runs/` producing graceful empty-state artifacts (committed per davi-2x2 convention), ruff clean on 7 new files, pytest 500 passed (test count unchanged — no new tests in P1a; parent's 480 reference was stale by 5).

**Outcome:**
- **Phase 1 acceptance gate met.** All three sub-blocks landed clean on `m8-3x3-eval-scaffold` over a single day. Test count over the block: **480 → 520 (+40)**. The 3x3 training stack now has the eval surface needed to observe Phase 2 — and the methodology in `experiments/davi-2x2/` extends to 3x3 without parallel-build.
- **Bounded V\* oracle is faster + smaller than planned.** Build wall-time **4.8s** on M4 Max (planned 30–120s). Cache **14.9 MB** at `data/v_star_bounded_3x3_k6.npz` (planned 60–80 MB). Both wins are because (a) batched fancy-index expansion via `apply_all_moves` is fast and (b) npz compresses int8/uint8 well. **Implication for backlog:** K=8 expansion is also cheap — we have headroom to extend the oracle if Phase 2 results show value in deeper sanity ground-truth.
- **P1c reshape — frozen npz → lean three-function API (recorded mid-block as the 2026-05-06 design pivot).** The original P1c was a 1400-state frozen `eval_set_3x3.npz`. Reshaped per user direction to three functions in `experiments/davi-3x3/eval.py`: `value_eval` (live every sync — random-walk forward pass + per-walk-depth distribution + per-V*-layer MAE against the oracle for d≤K + `macro_v_star_mae` driving early-stop), `beam_eval_walk` (post-training, random-walk × beam, **forever capability eval**), `beam_eval_v_star` (post-training, V*-stratified × beam, **temporary ground-truth eval while the bounded oracle exists**). No committed npz; deterministic-from-seed sampler. Plus `scripts/post_run_beam_eval.py` for post-hoc checkpoint trajectories. **Decision rationale (recorded):** beam search runs **post-training only** (beam cost would slow training); greedy is recovered as `beam_width=1`, no separate impl; intermediate checkpoints saved (`checkpoint_every`) make post-hoc trajectory analysis cheap; V*-layer discovery is dynamic so K=8 oracle expansion later just works without code changes.
- **Decision (recorded): no canonicalization for 3x3 V\*.** Unlike `v_star_2x2` which collapses the 24-rotation orbit, `v_star_bounded_3x3` uses raw `state.tobytes()` as the canonical key. Rationale: 3x3 centers are sticker-permutation-invariant under face turns, so the encoding is already canonical for QTM. The 2x2 needed canonicalization because it has no centers and is rotation-degenerate. Layer counts match the well-known QTM table exactly (1 / 12 / 114 / 1068 / 10011 / 93840 / 878880 → 983,926), confirming the simpler approach is correct. **Consequence:** the 3x3 V*-stratified sampler `sample_states_at_v_star_3x3` has no `rotate=` parameter (the 2x2's existed because its rotated-state orbit needed special handling for solver compatibility — irrelevant on 3x3).
- **Decision (recorded): early-stop measured in target-sync periods, not steps.** Captured in §Phase 2 of the revised plan. Between target-net syncs the Bellman target is frozen — loss is just settling toward a stationary target. The "real" progress signal is sync-to-sync. Phase 2 will validate this: 12 sync patience + 4 sync warmup + 0.001 min_delta on `macro_v_star_mae`. At sync_interval=500 → 6000 steps of plateau before stopping; generous, but catches the cycle-3/cycle-4 "10k+ flat steps" failure mode.
- **Decision (recorded): T0 capacity calibration deferred to backlog; Phase 2 uses hand-picked `[5120, 1024] × 4 bn` arch.** Acknowledged guess for traceability — explicitly NOT borrowed from DeepCubeA's `[5000, 1000]` (project rule: earn every hyperparameter). Rationale: 5120 = 5×1024, MPS-aligned; 3x3's input is wider (324 one-hot dims vs 144 on 2x2), so first hidden grows from 4096→5120 to acknowledge that without over-scaling. T0 sweep earns the real config when a downstream cycle calls for it.
- **Decision (recorded): separate `rubik-3x3` W&B project, distinct from 2x2's `rubik`.** Prevents cross-contamination of charts and runs; 2x2 rename to `rubik-2x2` is a backlog drive-by.
- **Eval API is pipeline-compatible by construction.** `value_eval` returns the dict-shaped record the existing `MetricLogger` + `WandbAdapter` already understands. W&B namespacing target: `eval/value/per_walk_depth/d{1..14}`, `eval/value/v_star_mae/d{1..6}`, `eval/value/macro_v_star_mae` (live); `eval/beam_walk/{solve_rate,mean_len}/d{1..14}`, `eval/beam_v_star/{solve_rate,mean_len,mae}/d{1..6}` (post-hoc). Phase 2 verifies wiring; no expected adapter changes.
- **`experiments/davi-3x3/run.py` partially disabled.** Old `eval_against_v_star` / `greedy_solve` call sites commented with `# P2a: replaced by value_eval — see plans/m8-3x3-davi.md` markers. `--help` parses, imports clean; full training wiring is P2a's explicit job. **Surgical patch — do not refactor run.py outside P2a's scope.**
- **Plan document revised on this branch.** `plans/m8-3x3-davi.md` rewritten mid-block to reflect the 2026-05-06 conversation pivots (lean eval API, T0 deferral, early-stop fields, W&B project split). Phase 3+ deliberately not committed-to in the plan — depends on Phase 2 outcomes.
- **Followups (noted as backlog or P2a scope, not opened as their own blocks):** (a) K=8 V\* expansion warm-started from K=6 (cheap given the 4.8s build); (b) T0 capacity sweep on 3x3; (c) 2x2 W&B project rename to `rubik-2x2`; (d) `audit_sampler.py` overflow-bucket charting once a real run produces walks beyond K=6.
- **Drive-by:** None on this block. The work was tightly scoped per sub-block.
**Commits:** `a25c09a` (block open), `9899a8f` (P1a scaffold), `2392af1` (P1a LOG), `11cac57` (P1b bounded oracle), `b8ea057` (P1b LOG), `0f65376` (plan revised mid-block), `619e90c` (P1c eval API), `e52598b` (P1c LOG), `<this commit>` (Outcome).

## 2026-05-06 — M8 3x3 bringup: CubeSpec + cubie oracle + tensor perm tables ✅ done
**Goal:** Mirror the 2x2 stack for 3x3 — add `CUBE_3X3` spec, extend the cubie oracle to 8 corners + 12 edges + (fixed) 6 centers, render a human-verifiable `visuals/oracle_rotations_3x3.html`, snapshot the 12-move sticker permutation table into `env.py`, and run the full equivalence + identities test corpus that the 2x2 stack passes. No training. Five separate branches stacked: `3x3-spec` → `3x3-cubie-oracle` → `3x3-oracle-viz` → `3x3-tensor-cube` → `3x3-equivalence`.
**Milestone:** M8 ([SPEC](SPEC.md#m8))
**Approach:** Block-per-branch off the prior block's tip. Each commits atomically with passing tests + ruff clean. Subagent per block, run sequentially overnight by parent agent. Concrete morning artifacts: passing test suite expanded with 3x3 equivalence/identity tests, `visuals/oracle_rotations_3x3.html` openable for visual confirmation of all 12 rotations, `MOVE_PERM_3X3` constant embedded in `env.py`.
**Next:** **Block closed.** All 5 branches stacked from main → 3x3-spec → 3x3-cubie-oracle → 3x3-oracle-viz → 3x3-tensor-cube → 3x3-equivalence. Ready for review or merge in the morning. Each branch's tip is a clean, atomic commit on top of passing tests.
**In progress:**
- B1 done: `CUBE_3X3` constant added to `src/rubik/cube/spec.py`. Tests in `tests/cube/test_spec.py` for 3x3 field values + solved_state. Branch `3x3-spec` off main HEAD `9ec8fed`.
- B2 done: cubie oracle extended for 3x3 (corners + 12 edges + fixed centers). U/D-axis edge-flip convention. Tests in `tests/oracle/test_cubie_3x3.py` cover M^4=I, sexy^6=I, Sune^6=I, T-perm^2=I, color preservation, random round-trip. Test count 269 → 322. Branch `3x3-cubie-oracle` off `3x3-spec`.
- B3 done: `visuals/oracle_rotations_3x3.html` rendered (375 KB, 4164 sticker rects across ~77 cube renderings). Sections: color legend, solved, 12 single-move applications, F-move edge-flip detail (3x3-only — the U/D-axis convention's distinguishing case), sexy^1, sexy^6 (24-step round-trip ending at solved), Sune (8-step), depth-10 scramble + inverse round-trip (seed 42). SVG renderer was already-parametric on `spec.size` — no refactor needed; 2x2 output verified byte-identical (129,703 bytes before and after). Branch `3x3-oracle-viz` off `3x3-cubie-oracle`.
- B4 done: `MOVE_PERM_3X3` (12, 54) derived from oracle and embedded in `src/rubik/cube/env.py`. `_MOVE_PERM` dispatch keys on `spec.name` (`"2x2"` → MOVE_PERM_2X2, `"3x3"` → MOVE_PERM_3X3). `scripts/move_perm_from_oracle_3x3.py` reproduces the table — mirrors the 2x2 derivation tool, walks the SOLVED render to build `(cubie_id, k) → sticker_idx` lookups for corners + edges + a face → sticker_idx lookup for centers, then re-renders post-move state to read off the perm. Tests in `tests/cube/test_env_3x3.py` verify shape (12, 54) + valid-permutation-per-row + all 6 centers (flat indices 4/13/22/31/40/49) fixed under all 12 moves + per-move tensor-vs-oracle equivalence for all 12 QTM moves + `apply_all_moves` shape + per-child match + batched apply + M^4=I via tensor + CW^3=CCW + 30-move random round-trip via tensor with inverse-reversed sequence. Test count 322 → 449 (mostly parametrize fan-out). Branch `3x3-tensor-cube` off `3x3-oracle-viz`.
- B5 done: 10k random-sequence oracle ↔ tensor equivalence corpus (depth 1..30, seed 42, runtime ~1.35s) + identities replicated against the tensor cube (M^4=I, M.M'=I, sexy^6=I, Sune^6=I, T-perm^2=I), color multiset preservation, `apply_all_moves` cross-check vs per-move on 50 random states, batched-apply equivalence on 64 random states × 12 moves. New file `tests/cube/test_env_3x3_equivalence.py` (31 tests). Test count 449 → 480. Branch `3x3-equivalence` off `3x3-tensor-cube`.

**Outcome:**
- **3x3 stack lands without 2x2 code-path branches.** The CubeSpec abstraction earned its keep: adding `CUBE_3X3` + extending the cubie oracle + snapshotting `MOVE_PERM_3X3` is *all* the production code that changed. `apply_moves`, `apply_all_moves`, `is_solved` dispatch on `spec.name` via the existing `_MOVE_PERM` dict; SVG and ASCII renderers were already parametric on `spec.size`.
- **Oracle convention pinned to U/D-axis edge-flip.** Edge orientation 0 = primary facet at slot's primary position. F/B turns flip; U/D/L/R don't. Hand-derived before coding (B2's report: identities all green first try). 8 corners reused unchanged from 2x2 (same 3-orientation convention). Centers positionally fixed under QTM face turns — encoded as just-the-color, indices `face_idx * 9 + 4`.
- **Visual artifact for human verification:** `visuals/oracle_rotations_3x3.html` (375 KB, 77 cube renderings, 4164 SVG cells) covers solved state, all 12 single-move applications, F-move edge-flip detail, sexy^6=I sequence, Sune sequence, depth-10 scramble + inverse round-trip. The "favorite pattern" — open with `open` + verify in seconds.
- **Test count: 264 → 480.** Tests added across all 5 blocks: spec invariants (B1), 3x3 oracle identities + cubie tensor (B2), per-move tensor equivalence + permutation validity + centers fixed + M^4=I (B4), 10k random-corpus oracle ↔ tensor (B5), batched-apply cross-check (B5), color preservation (B5).
- **MOVE_PERM_3X3 derivation reproducible:** `scripts/move_perm_from_oracle_3x3.py` mirrors the 2x2 derivation tool. Re-runs cleanly; emits the same constant the file embeds.
- **Decision (recorded): edge orientation convention is U/D-axis.** Standard convention for 3x3 — F/B turns flip, U/D/L/R don't. Hand-derived in B2; pinned by all the identity tests passing.
- **Decision (recorded): centers don't carry orientation under QTM.** No whole-cube rotations modeled (out of move set), so center cubies are positionally fixed. Encoded as just-the-color in `cubie_to_tensor_3x3`. M2 SPEC acceptance criterion would require revisiting if HTM is enabled later or whole-cube rotations are added.
- **Followups (not opened as their own blocks):** (a) the M8 milestone has more steps remaining — DAVI training on 3x3, beam search on 3x3, throughput verification with the 10M transitions/sec target — those are post-merge work, not part of this overnight bringup. (b) `tests/oracle/` could be parametrized over both specs to consolidate test files, but the 2x2 corner-only and 3x3 corner+edge cases diverge enough that the current side-by-side tests are clearer.
- **Drive-by (B1):** four 2x2 spec tests had been missing the `_2x2` suffix; renamed for symmetry with the new 3x3 ones.
- **Drive-by (B3):** confirmed SVG renderer (`src/rubik/viz/svg.py`) is fully parametric on `spec.size` — 2x2 output byte-identical pre/post 3x3 enablement (129,703 bytes). The viz milestone (M3) was done well.
**Commits:** d60d092 (B1 LOG open), 6d5de16 (B1 spec), 79edc67 (B2 oracle), 36f2eb4 (B2 tests + LOG), d053de5 (B3 render script), 72142a0 (B3 HTML + LOG), 835104a (B4 perm derivation + env wiring), 395710d (B4 tests + LOG), a822b4b (B5 equivalence tests), <B5 commit 2 — this commit>

## 2026-05-05 — W&B integration: opt-in-out sink alongside JSONL ✅ done
**Goal:** Add Weights & Biases as a parallel metrics sink so long runs (kmax28-class, 30k+ steps) can be watched in near real-time with the same charts the local analyzer produces. JSONL stays the source of truth; W&B is opt-in via config flag. No changes to trainer/oracle/eval logic — purely a sink addition leveraging the duck-typed `wandb_run` hook already wired into `MetricLogger` (`src/rubik/training/metric_logger.py:32-90`).
**Milestone:** drive-by (infra)
**Approach:** Branch `wandb-integration` from `main` HEAD `e304837`, parallel to the in-flight kmax28 cycle-4 work on `m5-cycle-4-kmax28-curriculum` (which keeps running untouched). Five small steps:
- **S1** — Config: add `wandb_enabled: bool`, `wandb_project: str`, `wandb_entity: str | None` to `DAVIConfig` (`src/rubik/training/config.py`). Update YAML round-trip + `from_yaml` schema. Tests for round-trip + default-disabled behavior. Confirm `wandb` package isn't yet a dep — `uv add wandb` once, with user approval (Installation Policy).
- **S2** — Init in `experiments/davi-2x2/run.py`: when `config.wandb_enabled`, lazy-import wandb, call `wandb.init(project=..., entity=..., name=<out_dir-stem>, dir=out_dir, config=asdict(config))`, pass `wandb.run` into `MetricLogger`. `wandb.finish()` on exit (try/finally).
- **S3** — Field-shape compat in the `MetricLogger` forward path: flatten dict-valued fields (e.g. `per_depth_mae`) into namespaced keys (`per_depth_mae/d1`, `per_depth_mae/d2`, ...) before calling `wandb_run.log()`. Local JSONL still gets the nested form (zero churn for existing analyzers).
- **S4** — Key namespacing for wandb auto-grouping: route metrics under `train/`, `eval/`, `eval/solve_rate/`, `eval/per_depth_mae/` prefixes keyed on the existing `event=` field. JSONL untouched.
- **S5** — Reproduce analyzer charts: doc the wandb workspace setup (panel groups matching `experiments/davi-2x2/analysis/analyze.py`'s 4 chart types — train-loss, macro-MAE, per-depth-MAE, solve-rate trajectories) in a checked-in `experiments/davi-2x2/wandb_workspace.md` so the layout can be recreated in <2 min on a fresh wandb account.
**Next:** S1 — extend `DAVIConfig` with the three wandb fields + round-trip tests. Ask user before `uv add wandb`.
**In progress:**
- Block opened. Branch `wandb-integration` from `main` HEAD `e304837`. Parallel to kmax28 cycle-4 (still running on `m5-cycle-4-kmax28-curriculum`, untouched). Out-of-scope: sweeps, artifact uploads, historical JSONL backfill — all noted as backlog candidates if W&B becomes the long-term home.
- **Deviation from S1 plan (recorded):** Original S1 added `wandb_enabled` / `wandb_project` / `wandb_entity` to `DAVIConfig`. Reversed — the "every YAML field required, no defaults" rule on `DAVIConfig` is load-bearing for hyperparam reproducibility (see `config.py:1-15`), and wandb is runtime plumbing, not science. wandb settings live as CLI args on `run.py` instead (`--no-wandb`, `--wandb-project`, `--wandb-entity`). DAVIConfig stays untouched.
- **Commit `5e825e9` — WandbAdapter sink + tests + uv add wandb.** New module `src/rubik/training/wandb_sink.py` provides `WandbAdapter` (wraps any `log(dict)`-shaped object) that transforms JSONL records into a chart-friendly W&B namespace policy: `train/` / `eval/` / `checkpoint/` / `run/` prefixes keyed on `event`, nested per-depth dicts flattened slash-separated, `_d<N>` patterns regex-regrouped to `eval/<base>/d<N>`. wandb only imported under `TYPE_CHECKING` — adapter testable without wandb installed (tests pass `MagicMock` through duck-typed surface). 11 new tests; training-suite test count 35 → 46. `uv add wandb` (0.26.1 + 17 transitive deps).
- **Commit `1880c01` — wire wandb into run.py + workspace doc.** `experiments/davi-2x2/run.py` gains `--no-wandb`, `--wandb-project` (default 'rubik'), `--wandb-entity`. wandb on by default; init failure (auth/network) prints stderr warning and falls back to JSONL-only. `wandb.finish()` runs in `finally` so partial runs close cleanly. `experiments/davi-2x2/wandb_workspace.md` describes panels mirroring `analysis/analyze.py`'s 4 chart types.
- Verified: `uv run pytest tests/training/ -x -q` → 46 passed; `uv run ruff check` clean on all touched files; `uv run python experiments/davi-2x2/run.py --help` shows the new flags. Did NOT launch a training run — left for user.
- **Pivot mid-block: wandb on by default with `--no-wandb` opt-out** (replaces original "opt-in via flag" framing). Adapter wiring same; default flipped + LOG title amended. Lower-friction for the user's actual use case (long runs they want to watch).
- **End-to-end smoke verified.** `experiments/davi-2x2/configs/smoke_wandb.yaml` (500 steps, tiny 0.47M-param net, batch=1024). Run via `uv run python ... run.py --config smoke_wandb.yaml` — wandb auto-loaded creds from `~/.netrc`, run live at https://wandb.ai/griffin-enterprises/rubik/runs/puj0oydj. All 5 event types reached W&B with correct namespacing: 1 run_start under `run/`, 20 step under `train/`, 6 eval under `eval/...` (incl. `eval/per_depth_mae/dN` flattened from dict + `eval/solve_rate/dN` regex-regrouped from `solve_rate_dN`), 2 checkpoint under `checkpoint/path`, 1 run_end. Sparkline summary in stdout confirmed step-indexed metrics rendering on the cloud side.

**Outcome:**
- **Wandb-on-by-default + JSONL-as-source-of-truth landed.** New `WandbAdapter` (`src/rubik/training/wandb_sink.py`, 148 lines) wraps any `log(dict)`-shaped object — duck-typed against `MetricLogger`'s existing `_RunLike` Protocol so the trainer's metric pipeline didn't need a single edit. 11 new tests at `tests/training/test_wandb_sink.py` (training suite 35 → 46). `experiments/davi-2x2/run.py` gains `--no-wandb`, `--wandb-project rubik` (default), `--wandb-entity` (default None / user's default entity); init failure degrades to JSONL-only with a stderr warning rather than aborting.
- **Decision: keep wandb settings out of `DAVIConfig`.** Original plan added `wandb_enabled` / `wandb_project` / `wandb_entity` as dataclass fields. Pivoted: the project's "every YAML field required, no defaults" rule (see `config.py:1-15`) is load-bearing for hyperparam reproducibility — every run's YAML must read as the full scientific story of how it was produced. wandb is runtime plumbing, not science. CLI args on `run.py` only. The infra-vs-hyperparam split stays clean.
- **Decision: namespacing/flattening lives in `WandbAdapter`, not `MetricLogger`.** Keeps the JSONL writer and the cloud forwarder fully decoupled. The adapter transforms event-tagged JSONL records into a chart-friendly W&B namespace policy: `event="step"` → `train/{loss,step_seconds,k_max,...}`; `event="eval"` → flat scalars under `eval/`, dict-valued fields recursively flattened slash-separated, `_d<N>` patterns regex-regrouped (so 14 sibling keys land as one "per-depth" panel group); `event="checkpoint"` → `checkpoint/path`; `event="run_start|run_end"` → `run/...`. JSONL on disk completely unchanged — every existing analyzer (`experiments/davi-2x2/analysis/analyze.py`, `scripts/eval_checkpoint.py`, `scripts/compare_evals.py`) keeps working with zero changes.
- **Decision: lazy wandb import inside the `if not args.no_wandb` block.** Means a `--no-wandb` run doesn't pay the wandb import cost (2-3s on cold cache). Means tests for `WandbAdapter` itself import only `TYPE_CHECKING`, so the unit tests run without wandb installed at all (relevant if anyone wants to skip wandb dep in a minimal env).
- **`wandb` added as runtime dep** (0.26.1 + 17 transitive). UV project-scoped install, reproducible from `uv.lock`. `wandb/` already in `.gitignore` (line 64), so the local sync subdir each run creates inside `runs/<run>/wandb/` is ignored automatically.
- **Smoke fixture left in repo:** `experiments/davi-2x2/configs/smoke_wandb.yaml` (500 steps, 0.47M-param net) stays as a reusable fast-debug config — useful any time we want to re-verify end-to-end wiring or check wandb namespacing on a new event type. Not science-bearing — clearly named.
- **Workspace doc:** `experiments/davi-2x2/wandb_workspace.md` describes the panel layout matching `analyze.py`'s 4 chart types so a fresh wandb account can recreate the view in <2 min.
- **Out of scope, noted for backlog:** W&B Sweeps (M7-shaped), artifact uploads (checkpoints, oracle dataset), historical JSONL backfill into wandb. None blocking.
**Commits:** `1a53d42` (block-open), `5e825e9` (sink + tests + uv add wandb), `1880c01` (run.py wiring + workspace doc), `d19ccc3` (S1-deviation note in LOG).

## 2026-05-05 — M5-followup cycle 4: K_max=28 + curriculum + reusable V*-aware eval ✅ done — failed experiment, archived
**Goal:** Address M6 deep-depth gap (d=10..14 misses ≥0.999 gate) by attacking the data-distribution constraint two ways from `sync500_kmax20-30k/net_final.pt`: (a) **K_max=28 warm-start** — flat raise of scramble depth, tests "more deep exposure alone"; (b) **K_max curriculum warm-start** — ramp K_max=14→28 over first 15k of 30k steps, hold at 28 for last 15k — tests "anchor low-end first, then expose deep" against the regression-to-mean concern (when training distribution skews deep, predictions cluster around mean). Co-deliverable: build a **reusable V*-aware eval harness** (initially conceived as `scripts/eval_checkpoint.py`) that takes any checkpoint and runs greedy + beam(width=256) across multiple sampling strategies (random-walk depth, true-V*-stratified) at depths 1..14, charting differences. Make it the project's standard evaluation pattern going forward.
**Milestone:** M5-followup ([backlog](ROADMAP.md#m5-followup)) — between M5 acceptance gate and M7
**Approach:** Branch `m5-cycle-4-kmax28-curriculum` from main HEAD `e304837`. Sequential per-stage: C1 K_max curriculum support; C2 V*-stratified sampler; C3 (initially) standalone eval harness — pivoted to extending `experiments/davi-2x2/analysis/` after user feedback (see Outcome §"Misfire"); C4 cycle-4 configs; C5 launch cell 1 with auto-cell-2 watcher; C7 eval + writeup.
**Next:** **Block closed.** Cycle 4 archived as a failed experiment. `kmax28_warm-30k/net_final.pt` is preserved on disk + git as a record but is **NOT** the new working baseline — `sync500_kmax20-30k/net_final.pt` (cycle 3 final) remains the project's checkpoint of record. Cell 2 (curriculum 14→28) was killed before it ran per user instruction after seeing cell 1's flat training trajectory; the curriculum hypothesis is unfalsified by data but is unlikely to fix the core regression mechanism (since cell 1's failure mode is "training on K_max=28 walks erodes deep-V* ordering," and a curriculum that ends at K_max=28 inherits the same end-state). Next session: pick a different lever from the M5-followup ROADMAP backlog — most likely V\*-stratified scramble resampling (Plan 2 in backlog, the most direct attack on the data-distribution constraint that doesn't share cycle-4's failure mode) or the failure-state diagnostic to further characterize what cycle-3's `sync500_kmax20-30k` is missing.
**In progress:**
- Block opened (`6aa08b8`). Branch `m5-cycle-4-kmax28-curriculum` from main HEAD `e304837`.
- **C1 done (`025af65`):** `DAVIConfig` extended with `max_scramble_depth_initial` + `max_scramble_depth_ramp_steps` (0/0 = flat behavior; ramp_steps>0 = linear interpolation from initial to max_scramble_depth over ramp_steps, clamped). `run.py` calls `config.current_k_max(step)` per training step and logs `k_max` in step records. 11 schedule tests; full suite 253 → 264. Cycle-4 configs `kmax28_warm.yaml` (flat K_max=28) and `kmax28_curriculum.yaml` (14→28 ramp over 15k) authored.
- **C2 done (`611d252`):** `sample_states_at_v_star` + `load_v_star_arrays` added to `oracle/v_star_2x2.py`. Sampler indexes the V* npz table by target depth, draws uniformly. Default `rotate=False` after smoke-test discovered rotated-solved-states fail the solver's strict `is_solved == spec.solved_state` check. 8 new tests; full suite 264 → 272.
- **C3 misfire (`afa62f9`, `cc6c918`):** Initial implementation built `scripts/eval_checkpoint.py` + `scripts/compare_evals.py` + a new standalone `cycle-4-cell1-vs-baseline.html` — a parallel pipeline duplicating the existing `experiments/davi-2x2/analysis/{analyze,capture_solve_histograms,render_error_trajectories}.py` capture-and-render coverage in a different visual format. User flagged the misfire ("That's not our process. The file is basically not useful, it's not our format. What happened here?") and the parallel pipeline was retracted. Methodology added to CLAUDE.md ("Cycle reporting pipeline (don't parallel-build)") + memory feedback. The library primitives from C2 stay; the parallel scripts deleted in C7h.
- **C5 done:** Cell 1 launched with `run_cycle4.sh` (detached watcher pattern from cycle 2). Started 04:00:38 UTC, finished 06:03:01 UTC (2h 02m wall, 245 ms/step at K_max=28, 30k steps). Cell 2 launch was killed by a stop-watcher within ~1 second of cell 1's `run_end` (per user instruction to inspect cell 1 before deciding cell 2). Cell 2 stub committed for the record (run_start + resume only; never ran a step).
- **C7 done (canonical pipeline path):** `analyze.py` (`1a95bcf`) extended to iterate over a RUNS list. `capture_solve_histograms.py` extended for cycle-4 + V\*-stratified strategy (`25ced5e`), then for greedy/beam method axis (`671c242`). `render_error_trajectories.py` extended for cycle-4 (`303f541`), then for method axis (`c11ea9f`). Schema: `runs[run][strategy][method].lens[d]`. Regenerated `error_trajectories.html` (9 H2 sections including beam histograms for both strategies) + `solve_histograms.json` (`27f7322`, `b3e8437`).

**Outcome:**
- **Cycle 4 verdict: failed experiment, archived, do not build on top of it.** Cycle-4 (`kmax28_warm-30k`, warm-start from `sync500_kmax20-30k`, K_max=28 fixed, 30k more steps) did NOT close the M6 deep-depth gap. **It opened a new gap.** v_star_stratified × beam(256) per-depth solve rate, cycle-3 → cycle-4: d=10 0.985→0.950 (-3.5pp), d=11 0.870→0.630 (**-24pp**), d=12 0.630→0.400 (**-23pp**), d=13 0.380→0.080 (**-30pp**), d=14 0.060→0.010 (-5pp). The 30k extra steps with K_max=28 actively HURT capability at the depths the cycle was designed to fix.
- **The "extra deep exposure alone" hypothesis is falsified.** Random-walk-depth=14 batches under K_max=28 land mostly on V*<14 states (walk redundancy), so the training data was not actually concentrated at the M6-gap depths. Worse, the broader walk distribution diluted the deep-V* ordering signal that cycle-3's narrower K_max=20 had built. Symptom: macro_mae improved (3.13 → 2.87) and pred_std widened (1.44 → 1.68) — the net got better at calibration in the bulk — while losing the fine-grained ORDERING the beam search consumes at deep V*.
- **macro_mae alone would have shipped this regression.** macro_mae looked great. The cycle-3-era eval shape (random_walk_depth × greedy at training time, n=50) showed only a -5pp regression at random_walk_depth d=14 beam — also misleading, since walk redundancy biases that population toward easier states. Only `v_star_stratified × beam(256)` surfaced the real -24 to -30pp regression. The cycle-4 finding **directly motivates** the methodology added to CLAUDE.md (right baseline = immediate predecessor; both strategies; both methods; macro_mae is calibration not capability — see "Evaluating training cycles correctly" bullet).
- **Cell 2 (curriculum 14→28) not run.** Killed before it ran per user instruction. The curriculum hypothesis ("anchor on shallow first to prevent regression-to-mean dilution") is unfalsified by data, but its end-state K_max=28 inherits cycle-1's failure mode — the network's terminal training distribution is still K_max=28 walks. Skeptical that curriculum alone would fix the deep-V* ordering loss.
- **Decision (recorded): `kmax28_warm-30k/net_final.pt` is NOT the new project baseline.** `sync500_kmax20-30k/net_final.pt` (cycle 3 final) remains the project's working DAVI checkpoint. The cycle-4 net is preserved as a record of "this lever doesn't work" but is do-not-use for downstream search work.
- **Decision (recorded): K_max>=20 with random-walk sampling is the wrong lever to attack the M6 deep-depth gap.** Cycle 1 (K=18) and cycle 3 (K=20) preserved deep-V* ordering; cycle 4 (K=28) destroyed it. The data-distribution problem identified in M5 cycle 2 ("random walks under-represent natural-state-distribution-mode true depths") cannot be fixed by raising K_max alone — wider K_max gives the bulk distribution at the cost of the tail. Future levers must directly target deep-V*-tail mass: V\*-stratified scramble resampling (M5-followup Plan 2 in ROADMAP) or curriculum-with-V\*-aware-final-distribution.
- **Co-deliverable: K_max curriculum schedule on `DAVIConfig`** (`025af65`). Implemented but never exercised end-to-end; available for future cycles. Schedule: linear interpolation from `max_scramble_depth_initial` to `max_scramble_depth` over `max_scramble_depth_ramp_steps` steps; flat-as-default (`0/0`).
- **Co-deliverable: `sample_states_at_v_star` + `load_v_star_arrays`** (`611d252`). Library primitives in `oracle/v_star_2x2.py`. Used by the canonical capture pipeline (C7b/C7e). Will also support V\*-stratified scramble resampling at training time (M5-followup Plan 2) when that block opens.
- **Co-deliverable: `experiments/davi-2x2/analysis/` pipeline now supports two new axes** — strategy (`random_walk_depth` / `v_star_stratified`) and method (`greedy` / `beam(256)`). Schema: `runs[run][strategy][method].lens[d]`. `error_trajectories.html` overlays all runs across 9 H2 sections (4 of which are histogram bands by `strategy × method`). All future cycle work uses this as the canonical comparison pattern.
- **Misfire (recorded as cautionary tale):** C3 / C3+ initially built a parallel report under `scripts/` with a sibling HTML in `results/`. Wrong: the project already had an analyze/capture/render layer; the right path was to extend it. CLAUDE.md gained a "Cycle reporting pipeline (don't parallel-build)" bullet documenting the convention; memory feedback recorded the lesson; the parallel scripts were deleted in C7h. The C3/C3+ commits remain in branch history as a record of "tried this, was wrong."
- **Methodology added to CLAUDE.md (`81b41eb`).** "Evaluating training cycles correctly" bullet codifies: right baseline = immediate predecessor (cycle N+1 vs cycle N, NOT vs cycle 1); both sampling strategies (walk redundancy hides deep-V* regressions that v_star_stratified surfaces); both solve methods (greedy 0% at deep V* hides ordering changes that beam reveals); macro_mae is calibration, not capability — verify on v_star_stratified × beam before accepting a cycle.
- **Drive-by:** `scripts/eval_checkpoint.py` and `scripts/compare_evals.py` deleted (added in C3/C3+, retracted in C7h). The `scripts/` dir is now back to its pre-cycle-4 contents.
- **Drive-by:** Wandb smoke-run output `experiments/davi-2x2/runs/smoke_wandb-500/` — surfaced as untracked in this branch's working tree because the wandb-integration smoke test ran in a separate shell while we were on this branch. Cherry-picked to main as `f479f76`; rebased out of cycle-4 (the commit was `34ff53d` pre-rebase; gone now). Belongs on main, doesn't belong on cycle-4.
- **Drive-by:** Backfilled `max_scramble_depth_initial: 0` and `max_scramble_depth_ramp_steps: 0` into all historical run-dir `config.yaml` snapshots so the new schema can load them. Schema-additive change; flat behavior for all pre-cycle-4 runs.
**Commits:** 6aa08b8 (open block), 025af65 (C1 curriculum + 11 tests), 611d252 (C2 V\*-stratified sampler + 8 tests), afa62f9 (C3 — retracted), cc6c918 (C3+ — retracted), 1a95bcf (C7a analyze.py iterate), 25ced5e (C7b capture cycle-4 + V\* strategy), 303f541 (C7c render cycle-4 + V\* band), 27f7322 (C7d regen artifacts), 671c242 (C7e capture method axis), c11ea9f (C7f render method axis), b3e8437 (C7g regen with method), 81b41eb (C7h cleanup + methodology), 83636db (C7i close LOG).

## 2026-05-05 — Restructure experiment dirs ✅ done
**Goal:** Give every `experiments/<name>/` dir the same shape — `analysis/` for post-hoc scripts (`analyze.py`, `render_*.py`, `audit_*.py`, `capture_*.py`), `results/` for writeups + artifacts (`results.md`, `intuition.md`, `*.html`, derived data files), top-level reserved for entry points (`run*`, `configs/`, `runs/`, `README.md`). Flatten `davi-2x2/davi-baseline/` up — the nested layer was speculative shared-infra ergonomics for a second davi experiment that never materialized.
**Milestone:** drive-by
**Approach:** Branch `experiments-restructure`. Single subagent does the moves with `git mv` (preserve history), updates path refs in scripts (renderer outputs, analyze inputs), resolves the two-`results.md` conflict in davi tree (parent is M5 milestone methodology → fold into README.md; child is single-run findings → goes to `results/results.md`). Smoke test: each renderer/analyzer at minimum syntax-checks under its new home; one full path-resolution test per dir if data is there.
**In progress:**
- Block opened. Branch `experiments-restructure` from `main` HEAD `ebc0053`.
- Subagent dispatched and completed: 4 experiment dirs restructured, davi-2x2 flattened. All `git mv` (history preserved). Path refs in 8 analysis scripts converted to `Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent` idiom. `run.sh` / `run_continue.sh` / config yaml `checkpoint_path` fields / README/results.md inline references updated to drop `davi-baseline/` segment and add `analysis/` prefix where needed. Smoke-tested every renderer + analyzer end-to-end (executed against real run dirs, not just syntax checks) — all wrote to correct `results/` paths.
- `.gitignore` flipped: `experiments/*/results/` removed (was treating `results/` as transient output, now committed by convention). `experiments/*/cache/` retained for future transient compute slot.
- Two-`results.md` resolution: parent `davi-2x2/results.md` (M5 methodology overview, tiers 0–3) folded into new `davi-2x2/README.md` along with the davi-baseline-30k rationale that was in the now-deleted `davi-baseline/README.md`. Child `davi-baseline/results.md` (single-run findings) moved to `davi-2x2/results/results.md`.

**Outcome:**
- **Consistent shape across all 4 experiment dirs.** `experiments/{batch-sensitivity-2x2,beam-search-2x2,davi-2x2,mps-methodology}/` now follow `README.md` + entry points + `configs/` + `runs/` at root, `analysis/` for post-hoc scripts, `results/` for writeups + visual artifacts. Tree post-restructure verified via `tree experiments/ -L 2`.
- **davi-2x2 flattened.** The `davi-baseline/` nesting layer was speculative shared-infra positioning that never paid off (only one davi experiment ever materialized under it). Eliminated. The shared infra (`eval.py`, `eval_set.npz`, `calibrate_step_time.py`, `run.py`) stays at the davi-2x2 root since that's where it's still imported from; if a second davi experiment ever appears, restructure then.
- **Path refs use file-relative idiom.** All moved scripts use `Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "<subdir>"` instead of cwd-relative paths — works regardless of where the script is invoked from. Decision: this is sturdier than `../...` strings and matches what the subagent found already in use; avoids brittleness from `cd`-into-experiment-dir assumptions.
- **`results/` is now committed by convention.** The previous `.gitignore` line `experiments/*/results/` reflected an old assumption that `results/` would hold transient run outputs. New convention: `runs/` holds run outputs (still fine to commit when small), `results/` holds writeups + checked-in visual artifacts (`*.html`, `solve_histograms.json`). Replaced gitignore line with explanatory comment + retained `experiments/*/cache/` slot for future transient compute.
- **Subagent flagged one pre-existing bug, untouched.** `experiments/beam-search-2x2/analysis/analyze.py` line 151 fails on `--acceptance-dir` with a relative path (`Path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)` requires absolute input). Pre-existing — same code, same issue, before the restructure. Left unfixed; recorded here for whoever next runs that flow.
- **Run-dir artifacts under `runs/*/config.yaml` etc. still embed old `davi-baseline/` paths.** These are run-time records (the actual paths used at run time), not active config — left untouched. New runs will use the new paths automatically.
- **No `src/` changes.** Restructure stayed within `experiments/`; package code untouched.
**Commits:** `8cec7ee`

## 2026-05-05 — M6 beam search (2x2) ✅ done with documented gap
**Goal:** Land batched beam search using V_θ as scorer with within-beam dedup, parametric on `CubeSpec`. Acceptance per SPEC: 100% solve on 1000 depth-≤14 scrambles at beam_width=256, mean solve length within 1 move of BFS-optimal. Bet: cycle-3's `sync500_kmax20-30k` net has good ordering signal (pred_std=1.51) even though greedy fails the M5 calibration gate — beam should consume that ordering. M5 declared done by user this session despite the gate; M6 either closes the capability gap or surfaces a substantive ordering-quality finding worth retraining for.
**Milestone:** M6 ([plan](plans/m6-beam-search.md)) — first cycle
**Approach:** Single branch `m6-beam-search` from `m5-davi` HEAD `14f54e4`. Five atomic commits:
- **C1** — `src/rubik/search/beam.py` (`beam_solve_batch`, `BeamSearchResult`) + tests in `tests/search/test_beam.py` mirroring `tests/solve/test_greedy.py` style. Sequential per-parent loop; batched inner expansion via `apply_all_moves`; raw-bytes dedup (not canonicalization — see plan §Decisions); back-pointer path reconstruction.
- **C2** — `experiments/beam-search-2x2/` scaffold + `BeamEvalConfig` dataclass at `src/rubik/search/config.py`.
- **C3** — First sweep run against `sync500_kmax20-30k/net_final.pt`: beam_widths=(1,4,16,64,256) × depths=1..14 × n_per_depth=200.
- **C4** — `analyze.py` + `render_beam_curves.py` + `beam_curves.html` + hand-written `intuition.md`.
- **C5** — Acceptance gate run: width=256, n_per_depth=1000, depths 1..14; pass = solve_rate ≥ 99.9% per depth AND mean V\*-excess ≤ 1.0.
**Next:** **Block closed.** User chose option (A) — accept M6 with documented gap, retrain in a separate followup. Three retraining backlog items added to ROADMAP (V\*-stratified resampling, curriculum scheduling, failure-state diagnostic). Next session: explore which retraining lever to open as a new block (likely the failure-state diagnostic first since it's <1 min compute and informs which of the two retraining strategies has more signal).
**In progress:**
- Block opened (`5687fa2`). Branch `m6-beam-search` from `m5-davi` HEAD `14f54e4`. Plan at `plans/m6-beam-search.md`.
- **C1 done (`fc81d87`):** `src/rubik/search/beam.py` lands `beam_solve_batch` + `BeamSearchResult`. Sequential per-parent loop; batched inner expansion via `apply_all_moves`; raw-bytes dedup keyed dict (best-V representative per unique state); back-pointer chain for path reconstruction; early exit on first solved child. Spec-agnostic. 12 new tests in `tests/search/test_beam.py` mirroring `tests/solve/test_greedy.py` style — width=1 matches greedy on a corpus, V*-oracle solves in V*(state) moves at width=4, width=k never worse than width=1 (treating -1 as +∞), emitted paths apply to reach solved, n_expansions counts beam_size×n_moves per inner step, error-case parity with greedy (wrong shape / negative max_steps / zero beam_width). Test count 238 → 250.
- **C2 done (`3cccd4f`):** `src/rubik/search/config.py` lands `BeamEvalConfig` (frozen dataclass, every field required, yaml round-trip with tuple preservation across `body_widths` / `depths` / `beam_widths`). 3 new tests (`tests/search/test_config.py`). `experiments/beam-search-2x2/` scaffold: `run.py` (loads config, dual-format checkpoint loader, iterates `(depth, beam_width)` cells with same scrambles per depth across widths for crisp cross-width comparison, logs JSONL `event="cell"` records, writes raw `solve_lens` + `v_star_excess` arrays to `solve_histograms_beam.json`); `run.sh`; `configs/sweep.yaml` (widths `(1,4,16,64,256)` × depths 1..14 × n_per_depth=200); `configs/acceptance.yaml` (width=256 × depths 1..14 × n_per_depth=1000 — SPEC's exact gate); `README.md` with earned-vs-first-try-defensible knobs; `results.md` / `intuition.md` skeletons. Test count 250 → 253. `analyze.py` + `render_beam_curves.py` deferred to C4 (they consume the metrics format defined in run.py).
- **C3 done (sweep run, no separate commit — committed with C4 at `19aa1e0`).** 70 cells in 334.1s wall against `sync500_kmax20-30k/net_final.pt`. At width=256: d=1..9 = 1.000, d=10 = 0.990, d=11 = 0.990, d=12 = 0.985, d=13 = 0.990, d=14 = 0.970. V*-excess all cells well under 1.0 gate (max 0.56, mean ~0.27). Width-vs-capability curve monotone-and-not-saturated at deep depths (d=14: w=1→0.365 → w=256→0.970, +9–14pp per 4× step). Capability is binding constraint, not solution length.
- **C4 done (`19aa1e0`):** `analyze.py` + `render_beam_curves.py` + `beam_curves.html` + hand-written `intuition.md`. `analyze.py` writes pivot tables (solve_rate, V*-excess, avg_solve_len, n_expansions, elapsed) + max-width acceptance-shape summary; folds `intuition.md` at end. `render_beam_curves.py` produces heatmap overview + banded 5/5/4 small-multiples (solve-rate / V*-excess / avg-len vs beam_width per depth) + solve-length histograms at max width. `intuition.md` lays out four hypotheses with verification plans (H1 width=256 1–2 doublings short, H2 deep-fail clusters at natural-distribution-tail true-V*, H3 better-trained V_θ closes gap without widening, H4 less-compressed V_θ amplifies beam lift). Run dir 348K committed.
- **C5 done (`445fce5`):** Acceptance run at width=256, n_per_depth=1000, depths 1..14, max_steps=20, 555.6s wall. Per-depth solve rate vs ≥0.999 gate: d=1..9 = 1.000 ✅, d=10 = 0.997 ❌, d=11 = 0.992 ❌, d=12 = 0.975 ❌, d=13 = 0.970 ❌, d=14 = 0.952 ❌. Failure count grows super-linearly past d=11 (3, 8, 25, 30, 48 per 1000). Mean V*-excess = 0.248 vs ≤1.0 ✅. **Overall: ❌ FAIL on solve-rate gate at d=10..14, ✅ PASS on V*-excess.** No `max_steps` saturation (d=14 avg solve length 9.16, plenty of headroom — failures are "never found a solved state in the beam"). C3 sweep at n=200 was systematically optimistic at the deep end by 1–2pp (d=14 sweep 0.970 vs acceptance 0.952). Methodology lesson recorded in intuition.md: when a sweep cell is within ~2× SE of a hard gate, rerun at the gate's n before declaring "almost there."
