- Drive-by: `.claude/` (local Claude Code harness state — `scheduled_tasks.lock` etc.) added to `.gitignore`.
- Drive-by: `_format_throughput` reuse fix in `analyze.py` (originally referenced a non-existent helper; now uses `fmt_throughput` consistently).
- Drive-by: macmon worked-example AGREE result (`gpu_busy=0.952 gpu_power=3.94W expected=1.000`) embedded in `mps-methodology/results.md` §5 as the calibration ground truth.
**Commits:** 17269cd (open), c33e772, d0aafcd, 990afd2, 63242a2, 3a0b530, fd3bf76, 4f8e677 (close), ee56b82 (merge)

## 2026-05-01 — M3 visualization stack (2x2) ✅ done
**Goal:** Land the human-verifiable layer for the 2x2: ASCII renderer, SVG render components (single state / sequence grid / side-by-side compare), and a static HTML view of canonical cube states. Renderers consume *notations* (face-dict), not raw tensors. Acceptance: snapshot tests green; user can `open` the HTML view and visually confirm solved, single-move, and depth-20 scramble states render correctly.
**Milestone:** [plans/m3-viz.md](plans/m3-viz.md)
**Approach:** Single branch `m3-viz`. New module `src/rubik/viz/` with `colors.py` (palette as single source of truth), `ascii.py` (unfolded cross, face-letter cells), `svg.py` (3 functions: `render_svg_state`, `render_svg_sequence`, `render_svg_compare` — all return SVG fragment strings, embeddable in HTML). Tests mirror prior milestones: exact-string asserts for ASCII, `pytest-snapshot` for SVG (snapshots under `tests/viz/snapshots/`). Static gallery `visuals/cube_examples_2x2.html` with sections: solved, single-move (R/U/F), depth-8 canonical sequence (sexy²), depth-20 scramble side-by-side with its inverse.
**Outcome:**
- Acceptance gate green: `135 passed in 1.46s`, `ruff check` clean, `ruff format --check` clean. New viz tests: 13 (4 ASCII, 4 colors+palette, 5 SVG including custom-palette test).
- 3 source files added (`src/rubik/viz/colors.py`, `ascii.py` ~70 lines, `svg.py` ~280 lines), `viz/__init__.py` filled out, `rubik/__init__.py` `__all__` extended. 3 test files + `tests/viz/snapshots/` (4 SVG snapshots committed). 2 visualization generators + 2 generated HTMLs in `visuals/`. 1 codegen script moved to top-level `scripts/`.
- HTML view opened cleanly; visual self-check passed (solved uniform per face, after-R cycle correct, depth-20 inverse round-trips to solved, aesthetic matches the oracle rotations view).
- **Decision (recorded):** Renderers consume *face-dict* notation (`dict[str, list[int]]`), never raw tensors. SPEC §4 rule. Callers with a tensor do `render_ascii(state_to_dict(state, spec), spec)` — one line, no convenience-wrapper duplication. Adding a new state representation only requires writing a converter in `notation/`, not touching the renderers.
- **Decision (recorded):** SVG output is byte-stable across runs — single spaces between attributes, deterministic attribute order via f-strings, face iteration via `spec.faces`. Composition via `x_offset`/`y_offset` arithmetic (flat `<rect>` + `<text>` elements), not `<g transform="translate(...)">`. Equally deterministic, simpler diffs, no transform stack to reason about. `pytest-snapshot` byte-compares without flake.
- **Decision (recorded):** ASCII layout indent = `2 * spec.size` (= face-width-in-chars + inter-face separator). Generalizes cleanly: 2 spaces for 2x2, 6 for 3x3 at M5. Whole renderer body is `spec.size`-parametric — drops in for 3x3 with no new code.
- **Decision (recorded):** ASCII tests use the "record once, comment the geometry reasoning" pattern (the strict alternative — hand-deriving expected strings from `FACE_SLOTS` + the M1 R-cycle — was over-budget for the value). Each test has an inline comment explaining which corners moved where; future engine drift fails meaningfully against the recorded string.
- **Decision (recorded):** Naming convention codified after user feedback — *purpose first, size variant trails*. Files like `oracle_rotations_2x2.html`, scripts like `render_oracle_rotations_2x2.py`, tests like `test_cube_basic_fields_2x2`. CLAUDE.md updated. Renamed: `cubie_2x2_rotations.html` → `oracle_rotations_2x2.html`; `cube_preview_2x2.html` → `cube_examples_2x2.html`; generators to `render_<what>_<size>.py`. Reasoning: search-friendly grep `2x2` still finds everything, *and* identifiers read as purpose-then-variant rather than purpose-fragmented-by-variant.
- **Decision (recorded):** "Preview" dropped as the umbrella term — implies a before/after, but these renders *are* the production output (no real version downstream). Prefer "view" / "examples" / "gallery" depending on what's being shown. The umbrella directory stays `visuals/` (for visualizations).
- **Decision (recorded):** One renderer for cube-as-pixels — SVG via `viz.svg.render_svg_state`. The previous CSS-grid `<div class="sticker">` path in the oracle rotations generator was dropped; both visualization scripts now call `render_svg_state` for the cube renders. Page-level CSS (body bg, fonts, section layout) stays per-script. Single source of truth for "how a cube state becomes pixels"; tweaks propagate to all visualizations on regen.
- **Decision (recorded):** Palette is pluggable — `SPEEDCUBING_PALETTE` is *one* named palette of type `Palette = dict[int, str]`, all SVG renderers take an optional `palette` kwarg defaulting to `SPEEDCUBING_PALETTE`. New palettes drop into `viz/colors.py` alongside; callers swap without editing files. Naming reflects "it's a palette," not "the palette" — easier to add `MUTED_PALETTE`, `HIGH_CONTRAST_PALETTE`, etc. later.
- **Decision (recorded):** `scripts/` (top-level) is the home for pure-code dev tools that don't produce a visual artifact (e.g. `move_perm_from_oracle_2x2.py` derives the move-perm Python literal from the oracle and prints it for paste-into-env.py). Distinct from `visuals/scripts/` which is reserved for HTML/SVG generators. The M1-follow-up's "reserves `scripts/` from becoming a junk drawer" was about avoiding it as a viz dir; with `visuals/` owning that role, `scripts/` is now properly the dev-codegen home.
- **Decision (recorded):** `[tool.basedpyright] pythonVersion = "3.12"` added to `pyproject.toml` so PEP 695 `type Palette = ...` alias syntax stops getting flagged as "requires 3.12 or newer." We're on 3.12 (`.python-version`); the analyzer just needed to be told.
- **Deviation (recorded):** Plan listed 8 atomic commits; 11 landed. 8 from the initial implementation pass + 2 from the user-feedback pass (renames + renderer consolidation; palette parameterization) + 1 from the naming-convention codification (`_2x2` at end). Honest evolution.
- **Deviation (recorded):** Plan section 3 of HTML view was titled "Depth-10 canonical"; actual sequence is 8 moves (`R U R' U' R U R' U'`) so renamed to "Depth-8 canonical (sexy²)". Plan section 2 said "3-frame"; landed 4-frame (solved + R + U + F). Plan typos, corrected at implementation.
- **Deviation (recorded):** `render_svg_compare` signature uses `top_labels` / `bottom_labels` (per-frame lists) instead of the plan's singular `top_label` / `bottom_label` — the plan's HTML preview spec already implied per-frame ("Top labels: [...]"), so we went with the consistent shape.
- **Deviation (recorded):** Color legend in `render_oracle_rotations_2x2.py` was rewritten to use 6 small inline `<svg><rect/></svg>` swatches per face (one per face) instead of styled `<div class="sticker">` elements, since the dropped CSS-grid sticker path took the `.sticker` styles with it. Could be hoisted to a helper or re-use `render_svg_state` over a synthetic single-face dict; left simple for now. Backlog candidate.
- Drive-by: `[tool.basedpyright]` config added — fixed the spurious type-alias diagnostic (above).
- Drive-by: `src/rubik/cube/env.py` module docstring + `MOVE_PERM_2X2` snapshot comment updated to point at the new `scripts/move_perm_from_oracle_2x2.py` location.
**Commits:** 8d55969 (open), 434f206, 4c2769b, dec849b, d8a3a7b, 9415eb3, cd1f929, e8677b0, 966fa4e, dae822a (atop M2's `b4509b0` merge)

## 2026-05-01 — M2 fast tensor cube (2x2) ✅ done
**Goal:** Land the vectorized tensor cube for the 2x2. Batched `apply_moves`, `apply_move_sequence`, `is_solved`, `random_scrambles`, `valid_next_moves_mask` in `src/rubik/cube/env.py`. Move tables snapshotted from the M1 oracle (no runtime dep). Acceptance: all 2x2 identities; tensor ≡ oracle on 10k random sequences; inverse-of-scramble round-trip solves.
**Milestone:** [plans/m2-tensor-cube.md](plans/m2-tensor-cube.md)
**Approach:** Single branch `m2-tensor-cube`. State = flat `int8` sticker tensor `(spec.n_stickers,)` or batched `(B, n_stickers)`, exactly `CubeSpec.solved_state` shape. Move table = `(12, 24)` int64 permutation; apply via `torch.gather(states, -1, perm[move_idxs])`. Generate the snapshot offline via `visuals/scripts/generate_move_perm_2x2.py` (fingerprint renderer over the oracle); paste the literal into `env.py`; `_MOVE_PERM[spec.name]` dispatch keeps the code path 2x2/3x3 generic. Module-level functions, no `Cube` class. Device-agnostic, dtype-preserving. Tests mirror M1's identity suite + `EXPECTED_CW_POSITIONS` direction check, add 10k oracle-equivalence sweep, batched-apply, random-scrambles + same-face-prune, mask correctness, dtype/device preservation.
**Outcome:**
- Acceptance gate green: `122 passed in 1.41s`, `ruff check` clean, `ruff format --check` clean. New cube tests: 37 across all 15 plan sections (1 over plan: an explicit `test_random_scrambles_no_prune_allows_same_face` to cover the `prune_same_face=False` branch).
- 3 source files added (`src/rubik/cube/env.py` 208 lines, `tests/cube/test_env.py` 195 lines, `visuals/scripts/generate_move_perm_2x2.py` 112 lines), 2 modified (`src/rubik/cube/__init__.py` from empty to re-export hub, `src/rubik/__init__.py` extended `__all__`), plan + LOG/ROADMAP committed in the open commit.
- 10k oracle-equivalence sweep (depths 1–30) runs in well under 1 second on CPU — left at 10k as planned. Plenty of headroom to scale up if M5/M6 wants it; explicitly *not* a perf measurement.
- **Decision (recorded):** Tensor cube does **not** import the oracle at runtime. Move tables are static int64 permutations baked into `env.py` from a one-shot generator script (`visuals/scripts/generate_move_perm_2x2.py`). Drift is caught in CI by `test_move_perm_matches_oracle`, which re-derives the table inline and compares to the embedded constant. Implements SPEC.md line 230 ("*not* a runtime dependency on the oracle"). Generator lives under `visuals/` because it's a dev-time tool producing an out-of-tree artifact (the snapshot); fits the M1-follow-up convention of "artifact + generator both checked in."
- **Decision (recorded):** State representation = flat `int8` sticker tensor matching `CubeSpec.solved_state` shape `(spec.n_stickers,)` for single, `(B, spec.n_stickers)` for batched. No `Cube` class wrapper; module-level functions only. Tensors are the wire format (parity with M0/M1, parity with `notation/state.py` and `oracle/cubie.py`'s style). If a downstream phase wants method-style syntax we can wrap then; YAGNI now.
- **Decision (recorded):** `apply_moves` workhorse uses `torch.gather(states, -1, perm[move_idxs].to(states.device))`. Single int / 0-d tensor / 1-d tensor of move indices all flow through; broadcasts cleanly to `(S,)` or `(B, S)` states. Device-agnostic and dtype-preserving via gather semantics. No MPS-specific code at this milestone — that's M4's experiment loop.
- **Decision (recorded):** `_MOVE_PERM` dispatch dict keyed by `spec.name` (`"2x2"` for now, `"3x3"` drops in at M5 via `generate_move_perm_3x3.py` + paste). The "no 2x2/3x3 forks" rule from SPEC decision 1 is satisfied at the code level: same `apply_moves` body serves both sizes; only the data table differs. No size branching.
- **Decision (recorded):** `random_scrambles` defaults `prune_same_face=True` since DAVI / scramble generation typically wants it; the prune is implemented per-step via `torch.multinomial` on a per-row probability vector that zeros out same-face entries given the previous move. Tested both branches.
- **Deviation (recorded):** Plan listed 6 atomic commits; 7 landed because ruff lint/format fixups for the long-line `noqa: E501` markers on the 24-int snapshot rows + import-sort autofix were split into their own commit (`6536cef`) to keep the env.py and re-export commits clean. The `# fmt: off` / `# fmt: on` block + per-row noqa preserves single-line scannability of the snapshot, which would otherwise fragment under 88-column splitting and lose readability.
- **Deviation (recorded):** One extra test beyond the 15 in the plan (`test_random_scrambles_no_prune_allows_same_face`) — explicit coverage for the `prune_same_face=False` branch of `random_scrambles`. Without it that path was untested.
- Drive-by: `is_solved` returns a 0-d bool tensor for single-state input and `(B,)` for batched; tests use `bool(...)` / `.item()` to scalarize where needed. Same shape semantics as PyTorch's `.all(dim=-1)` reduction.
**Commits:** f379ea1, 5de9312, 8a2b198, 292b419, 6536cef, d60935d, 4b23a3f (atop opening commit 88dbf7b)

## 2026-05-01 — M1 follow-up: R direction fix + 2x2/3x3 naming + visuals/ ✅ done
**Goal:** Fix the R move (CW cycle was reversed — physically R'; user spotted via visual preview). Add direction-sensitive regression tests. Codify the **cube/cubie + `_2x2`/`_3x3` naming convention** in `CLAUDE.md` (search-friendly: `grep "2x2"` finds all 2x2-specific identifiers; `cube`/`cubie` stay generic). Reorganize HTML visualization artifacts into `visuals/` at the repo root with a `generate_<thing>.py` script convention.
**Milestone:** drive-by (M1 follow-up)
**Approach:** Single branch `cubie-r-direction-fix`. (1) Move files: `scripts/` → `visuals/`, with generators under `visuals/scripts/generate_<thing>_2x2_<thing>.py`. (2) Fix `_CW_MOVES["R"]` in `oracle/cubie.py`: invert the cycle (`URF → URB → DRB → DRF`) with inverse orientation deltas. (3) Add `test_cw_direction` parametrized over all 6 faces with hand-derived expected `state.positions`. (4) Apply naming convention to new identifiers (`generate_cubie_2x2_rotations.py`, `cubie_2x2_rotations.html`); leave `CUBE_2X2` as-is (already correct). (5) Add the `_2x2`/`_3x3` convention to `CLAUDE.md` so future code follows it. (6) Regenerate HTML, visually verify R now goes the right way.
**Outcome:**
- All gates green: `85 passed`, `ruff check` clean, `ruff format --check` clean. 6 new directional tests added (`test_cw_direction`, parametrized per face).
- File reorganization: `scripts/preview_rotations.py` → `visuals/scripts/generate_cubie_2x2_rotations.py`; `scripts/cubie_rotations.html` → `visuals/cubie_2x2_rotations.html`; `scripts/` removed.
- `_CW_MOVES["R"]` inverted: was `2→6→7→3→2` (physically R'), now `2→3→7→6→2` (physically R CW per right-hand-side viewing geometry). Orientation deltas inverse-flipped to match; new pattern `(1, 2, 1, 2)` around the new cycle.
- **Decision (recorded):** Cube/cubie + `_2x2`/`_3x3` naming convention — `cube` and `cubie` are generic (apply to both sizes); size-specific identifiers carry `_2x2` or `_3x3`. Goal: `grep "2x2"` finds every 2x2-specific identifier (constant, file, test function, etc.). Verbose but unambiguous and search-friendly; the alternative shorter `2`/`3` suffix collides with version numbers and other unrelated numerics. `CUBE_2X2` was already on this convention — kept as-is. Documented in `CLAUDE.md`. (User flip-flop history: briefly tried `CUBE2`/`cubie2` form before reverting to `_2x2` for searchability.)
- **Decision (recorded):** R-direction bug originated in my M1 plan — I prescribed the wrong cycle (`URF → DRF → DRB → URB`) for R CW. The brute-force search at implementation time produced consistent orientation deltas around that wrong cycle, and the algebraic identities (M⁴=I, Sune⁶=I, sexy⁶=I) are mirror-symmetric under R↔R' so they didn't catch it. The visual preview surfaced it within minutes — this validates the static-HTML preview pattern as a correctness check for cube-geometry code.
- **Decision (recorded):** Direction-sensitive testing — algebraic identities don't catch R↔R' inversion. The new `EXPECTED_CW_POSITIONS` table (6 hand-derived position permutations) + `test_cw_direction` is the only test in the suite that catches direction errors. M2's tensor cube must satisfy this same table.
- **Decision (recorded):** `visuals/` folder for human-verifiable HTML/SVG artifacts (out-of-tree, dev-time). Distinct from `src/rubik/viz/` (production renderers, M3+). Generators co-located in `visuals/scripts/generate_<thing>.py`. Both artifact and generator checked in. Reserves `scripts/` from becoming a junk drawer.
**Commits:** dc31a4a, 2b92ffa

## 2026-05-01 — M1 cubie oracle ✅ done
**Goal:** Hand-rolled cubie oracle for the 2x2 cube — corner state as `(positions, orientations)`, moves applied as physical rotations, sticker rendering. Pins within-face geometric ordering. Acceptance: M⁴=I, (RUR'U')⁶=I, Sune⁶=I, M'=M³, color-multiset preservation, 1000-step random walk without divergence.
**Milestone:** [plans/m1-cubie-oracle.md](plans/m1-cubie-oracle.md)
**Approach:** Frozen `CornerState` dataclass holding two 8-tuples. Bit-packed slot numbering `(U/D, L/R, F/B)`. U/D-axis orientation reference. Hand-write 6 CW move tables `dict[face, tuple[(slot_before, slot_after, ori_delta)*4]]`; derive CCW as CW³. `cubie_to_tensor(state, spec) -> torch.Tensor[24]` lives in `oracle/cubie.py` (cubie struct is internal, not a notation). Single test file `tests/oracle/test_cubie.py`. No new deps. Branch: `m1-cubie-oracle`.
**Outcome:**
- Acceptance gate green: `79 passed`, `ruff check` clean, `ruff format --check` clean. Oracle tests: 37 (10 categories, several parameterized over moves/faces).
- 4 source files added (`src/rubik/oracle/cubie.py` 207 lines, `src/rubik/oracle/__init__.py`, `tests/oracle/__init__.py`, `tests/oracle/test_cubie.py` 97 lines), 1 modified (`LOG.md`), plan committed to `plans/m1-cubie-oracle.md`.
- **Decision (recorded):** Within-face sticker layout pinned per the plan's `FACE_SLOTS` table — row-major reading order viewed from outside, with per-face "up" axes (U toward B, D toward F, side faces toward U). All downstream consumers (M2 tensor cube, M3 renderer) inherit this.
- **Decision (recorded):** Slot numbering bit-packed `(U/D, L/R, F/B)` — slot 0=ULF..7=DRB. Internal-only; not exposed in any user-facing API. Pays off in mask-based U-row vs D-row logic.
- **Decision (recorded):** CCW moves derived as `CW³` (composed from CW), not hand-written. The `M' = M³` test thereby reduces to "CW table is internally consistent" — fine because CW correctness is also covered by M⁴=I, (RUR'U')⁶=I, and Sune⁶=I.
- **Deviation (recorded):** The plan's `SLOT_FACETS` table prescribed `(UD, FB, LR)` ordering uniformly across all 8 slots. That labeling does not satisfy the algebraic identities under any consistent orientation-delta assignment — verified by hand-derivation, then by brute-force search over (SLOT_FACETS labeling × side-face orientation patterns). Final labeling is per-slot specific (4 of 8 slots differ from the plan): rotation geometry forces it, equivalent to standard speedcubing convention up to choice of "first side facet" at each slot. Captured in code comments in `oracle/cubie.py`.
- **Decision (recorded):** Side-face moves use `(1, 2, 1, 2)` orientation-delta pattern uniformly around each cycle (not a mix of patterns across L/F/R/B). Cleaner than the prompt's initial `(2, 1, 2, 1)` for R/B — both are mathematically valid but the uniform pattern was chosen for symmetry with the SLOT_FACETS labeling.
- **Decision (recorded):** `cubie_to_tensor` lives in `oracle/cubie.py`, not in `notation/`. Cubie struct is the oracle's internal representation; not promoted to a user-facing notation. `tensor_to_cubie` deliberately not implemented (sticker→cubie inversion non-trivial; M1 acceptance doesn't need it).
- **Decision (recorded):** `apply_move` raises `ValueError` on out-of-range move index — boundary validation, even though internal callers won't hit it.
- Drive-by: module-import asserts on `_CW_MOVES` (cycle is permutation, deltas sum to 0 mod 3) — cheap typo-catcher, runs once at import.
**Commits:** 5d86d55

## 2026-05-01 — M0 skeleton ✅ done
**Goal:** Land the package skeleton, `CubeSpec` abstraction, and notation hub stubs (move + state converters with round-trip tests). Acceptance: `uv run pytest` green, `uv run ruff check` clean, `CubeSpec(CUBE_2X2)` round-trips through every notation converter.
**Milestone:** [plans/m0-skeleton.md](plans/m0-skeleton.md)
**Approach:** Create the 7 package subdirs as `__init__.py` placeholders. Implement `cube/spec.py` (CubeSpec dataclass + `CUBE_2X2`), `notation/moves.py` (CubeSpec-independent QTM converters), `notation/state.py` (CubeSpec-parameterized tensor↔face-dict). Add ruff + pytest config to `pyproject.toml`. Tests under `tests/cube/` and `tests/notation/`. Branch: `m0-skeleton`.
**Outcome:**
- Acceptance gate green: `42 passed`, `ruff check` clean, `ruff format --check` clean.
- 17 source files added (7 skeleton `__init__.py`, 3 content modules, 5 test files + 2 empty test `__init__.py`), 2 modified (`pyproject.toml`, `src/rubik/__init__.py`), plan committed to `plans/m0-skeleton.md`.
- **Decision (recorded):** Face order locked at `("U","L","F","R","B","D")` — inherited from archived draft (lines 50–55) so the future encoding doc / 3x3 extension stays consistent. Move index mapping locked at `face_idx * 2 + direction` (CW=0, CCW=1) so same-face pruning is `move >> 1`.
- **Decision (recorded):** Within-face geometric ordering deliberately NOT pinned at M0. M0 commits only to face-block ordering and contiguity; M1's cubie oracle pins the within-face mapping when it generates physical-rotation move tables.
- **Decision (recorded):** `CubeSpec` kept lean — no `move_table`, no `face_to_color_map`, no corner/edge bookkeeping. Fields earn their way in by being needed downstream (move_table at M2, edge bookkeeping at M5 if oracle architecture demands).
- **Decision (recorded):** `solved_state` is a `@property` returning a fresh tensor each call (no caching). Avoids aliasing surprises if a caller mutates; cost is negligible (24-element int8 tensor). Locked in via a `test_solved_state_returns_fresh_tensor`.
- Drive-by: `state_to_dict` validation extended to also cover bad-shape inputs (plan only specified bad-input cases for `dict_to_state`).
**Commits:** 164e0da

## 2026-05-01 — Bootstrap project ✅ done
**Goal:** Initialize the repo, package skeleton, foundational docs, and first commit.
**Milestone:** drive-by (pre-M0)
**Approach:** `git init`, `uv init --lib`, add deps, write SPEC / ROADMAP / CLAUDE / LOG, archive the original draft spec, commit.
**Outcome:**
- `git init -b main`; default branch `main`.
- `.gitignore` covering Python, macOS, IDE, env, and project-specific (model checkpoints, training logs, `experiments/*/runs/`, `experiments/*/results/`).
- `uv init --lib --no-readme --python 3.12` produced `src/rubik/` package with `py.typed`. Trimmed auto-generated `authors` and updated `description` in `pyproject.toml`.
- Deps: runtime `torch`, `numpy`; dev `pytest`, `ruff`, `pytest-snapshot`. No pycuber (3x3-only, unmaintained); no tensorboard / wandb / matplotlib (deferred).
- Authored `CLAUDE.md` (opts into cc-process via `@~/.claude/cc-process.md`, codifies tooling + key conventions), `SPEC.md` (project goal, 8 key decisions with rationale, M0–M9 acceptance criteria, package layout, references), `ROADMAP.md` (cc-process format), `LOG.md` (this file).
- Original draft moved to `plans/archive/llm-draft-spec.md` — retained for technical detail (sticker indexing, network architecture, hyperparam starting points), no longer authoritative.
- **Decision (recorded):** 2x2 first, parameterized via `CubeSpec` — single code path, no 2x2/3x3 forks. Two-witness correctness (cubie oracle + tensor); pycuber dropped. QTM only at first. Notation hub as a first-class module. Visualization is its own milestone (M3) with HTML/SVG preferred over matplotlib. "Make it work, then make it fast" — perf bar moves to M4. Experiment-loop pattern established at M4, reused at M8.
**Commits:** 9522ce0
